01-01.01_项目介绍

欢迎参加微软全栈开发工程师专业认证课程。

在本课程中，您将掌握从零开始构建动态、完整应用程序所需的核心技能。

您将从编码基础开始，学习C#编程，并使用Blazor和Web技术创建引人入胜的用户界面。

您还将获得管理数据库、使用.NET进行服务器端编程的专业知识，并运用最佳实践在身份验证和安全性方面保护您的应用程序。

整个学习过程都融入了微软Copilot，它将帮助您简化编码、调试和优化工作。

在此过程中，您将提升性能、确保可扩展性，并探索使用开发运维工具进行基于云的部署。

完成这包含一个毕业设计项目在内的12门课程后，您将获得成为熟练全栈开发人员所需的实践经验和技术知识。

现在，让我们来了解一下本课程包含的具体科目。

我们将从全栈编码基础开始，您将在此学习如何规划和构建项目、进行逻辑思考并解决问题。

您将设计算法，实现控制结构，并开发模块化、可重用的代码。

在整个课程中，微软Copilot将协助您进行编码和调试。

在《C#编程入门》课程中，您将在Visual Studio Code中设置.NET项目，并学习关键的编程概念，如变量、循环、方法和面向对象原则。

您还将接触异步编程，并使用Copilot来编写和优化您的C#代码。

接下来，在《Web开发入门》课程中，您将学习使用HTML、CSS和Java Script构建网页的基础知识，使其具有响应性且易于访问。

您还将使用Git Hub进行协作，并在Copilot的协助下开发一个小型的交互式网站。

然后，在《使用Blazor Pages进行前端开发》课程中，您将使用Blazor创建动态网站，学习使用组件和数据绑定。

您将构建一个Blazor项目，管理应用程序数据，并使用Copilot来提升您网站的效率和用户体验。

接下来进入使用.NET的后端开发，您将使用.NET框架，通过ASP.NET Core创建Web API，并集成Open API以生成文档。

您将应用C#，并使用Copilot来实现和优化API代码，以实现无缝的后端开发。

在数据库集成与管理课程中，您将使用Entity Framework Core或称EF Core来处理关系型数据库。

您将设置数据库、为数据建模，并使用SQL进行查询和数据操作，同时Copilot会帮助您优化SQL查询的性能和安全性。

在全栈集成课程中，您将结合前端和后端技能，设置全栈开发环境，连接API，并管理数据流。

为确保集成顺畅，您还将探索Blazor的API处理方法，并使用Copilot构建一个全栈项目。

接下来是安全性与身份验证，您将在此学习如何使用ASP.NET身份验证框架和基于角色的访问来保护应用程序。

内容涵盖用户注册、基于令牌的身份验证、加密以及JSON Web令牌或称JWT。

您将使用Copilot编写安全的代码并保护用户数据。

在性能优化与可扩展性课程中，您将专注于通过缓存、查询优化和负载均衡来提升应用程序性能。

Copilot将帮助您微调代码和数据库查询，以应对增加的流量并提高效率。

接着是数据结构与算法，您将探索处理和组织数据的最有效方法。

您将实现数组、链表、树以及排序和搜索等算法来解决后端问题，同时使用Copilot优化性能。

接下来，在部署与开发运维课程中，您将学习如何使用Azure云服务部署和管理您的应用程序。

您将设置CI-CD流水线、自动化任务并监控性能，Copilot将帮助您简化部署活动。

最后，您将在毕业设计项目中把所有知识融会贯通，构建一个完整的全栈应用程序。

您将集成前端和后端组件，优化性能，并展示您管理实际开发工作流的能力。

完成本课程后，您将掌握关键的全栈开发技能，从前端技术如HTML、CSS和Blazor，到后端框架、数据库和API集成。

您将能够构建可扩展且安全的Web应用程序并优化其性能，同时Copilot将帮助您简化和增强开发过程。

我们非常激动能引导您踏上这段成为全栈开发工程师的旅程。

英文原文

Welcome to the Microsoft Full Stack Developer Professional Certificate.

In this program,you'll master the skills needed to build dynamic,full-scale applications from the ground up.

You'll start with coding foundations,learn C#programming,and create engaging user interfaces with Blazor and web technologies.

You'll also gain expertise in managing databases,handling server-side programming with.NET,and securing your applications using best practices in authentication and security.

With Microsoft Copilot integrated throughout,you'll streamline coding,debugging,and optimization.

Along the way,you'll enhance performance,ensure scalability,and explore cloud-based deployment using Dev Ops tools.

By the end of these 12 courses,including a capstone project,you'll have the practical experience and technical knowledge to become a proficient full-stack developer.

Let's explore the courses in this program.

We will begin with the foundations of coding full stack,where you'll learn to plan and structure projects,think logically,and solve problems.

You'll design algorithms,implement control structures,and develop modular,reusable code.

Microsoft Copilot will assist in coding and debugging throughout the course.

In Introduction to Programming with C#,you'll set up.NET projects in Visual Studio Code and learn key programming concepts like variables,loops,methods,and object-oriented principles.

You'll also work with asynchronous programming and use Copilot to write and optimize your C#code.

Next,in Introduction to Web Development,you'll learn the basics of building web pages using HTML,CSS,and Java Script,making them responsive and accessible.

You'll also collaborate using Git Hub and develop a small interactive website with Copilot's assistance.

Then in Blazor Pages for Front-End Development,you'll use Blazor to create dynamic websites,working with components and data binding.

You'll build a Blazor project,manage app data,and use Copilot to enhance your website's efficiency and user experience.

Moving on to backend development with.NET,you'll work with the.NET Framework,creating web APIs using ASP.NET Core and integrating Open API for documentation.

You'll apply C#and use Copilot to implement and optimize API code for seamless back-end development.

You'll work with relational databases in database integration and management using Entity Framework Core or EF Core.

You'll set up databases,model data,and use SQL for queries and data manipulation,while Copilot helps you optimize SQL queries for performance and security.

In full stack integration,you'll combine your front-end and back-end skills,setting up a full-stack environment,connecting APIs,and managing data flow.

To ensure smooth integration,you'll also explore Blazor API handling and build a full stack project with Copilot.

Next is security and authentication where you'll learn to secure applications using ASP.NET identity and role-based access.

You'll cover user registration,token-based authentication,encryption,and JSON Webs Tokens or JWT.

You'll use Copilot to write secure code and protect user data.

In performance optimization and scalability,you'll focus on improving application performance through caching,query optimization,and load balancing.

Copilot will help you fine-tune your code and database queries to handle increased traffic and efficiency.

Then in data structures and algorithms,you'll explore the most efficient ways to handle and organize data.

You'll implement arrays,linked lists,trees,and algorithms like sorting and searching to solve back-end problems,all while optimizing performance with Copilot.

Next,in deployment and Dev Ops,you'll learn how to deploy and manage your applications using Azure Cloud Services.

You'll set up a CI-CD pipeline,automate tasks,and monitor performance with Copilot helping you streamline your deployment activities.

Finally,you'll bring everything together in the Capstone project by building a comprehensive full stack application.

You'll integrate front-end and back-end components,optimize performance,and demonstrate your ability to manage real-world development workflows.

By the end of this program,you'll have mastered key full-stack development skills from front-end technologies like HTML,CSS,and Blazor to back-end frameworks,databases,and API integration.

You'll be able to build scalable,secure web applications and optimize their performance,with Copilot helping you streamline and enhance your development process.

We're excited to guide you on this journey towards becoming a full-stack developer.

01-01.02_课程介绍-编码基础-全栈开发

欢迎学习全栈编码基础课程。

在这里，您将掌握构建和管理Web应用每一层的技能，从动态的前端界面到强大的后端系统。

我们将深入探讨API集成、版本控制和安全性最佳实践，为您提供创建健壮、可扩展应用程序的工具。

课程结束时，您将拥有将全栈项目变为现实的技能和信心，并在当今技术驱动的世界中茁壮成长。

我们将从审视全栈开发开始，揭示它如何结合前端和后端的专业知识来创建无缝的应用程序。

我们将探讨全栈开发人员的独特角色，以及使他们在行业中不可或缺的关键工具和技术。

接下来，我们将加强您的问题解决能力，学习以清晰的逻辑和战略思维来应对挑战。

我们将探索强大的技术来分解复杂任务，并编写可用于实际场景的高效代码。

然后，我们将探讨客户端-服务器架构、HTTP和HTTPS之间的区别，以及RESTful API和Graph QL的原则。

我们还将研究如何使用Web Sockets进行实时通信，以增强动态应用程序的交互性。

接着，我们将探讨数据库的基础知识，研究关系型和非关系型结构。

您将学习如何有效地设计和管理数据库模式，然后专注于优化数据库性能的策略，以实现最高效率。

接下来，我们将介绍版本控制的要点，探索用于协作的Git和Git Hub，并深入研究Git的高级技术。

您将应用最佳实践为全栈应用程序创建概念设计。

最后，您将应用编码概念和问题解决技术，创建算法和流程图，使用控制结构和循环，并开发模块化代码，最终完成一个综合性的编程项目。

在整个课程中，您将掌握全栈开发，从构建前端界面到管理后端系统。

您将学习集成API、优化数据库和使用版本控制，使您能够创建动态、可扩展的应用程序。

我们很高兴能引导您成为一名熟练的全栈开发人员。

英文原文

Welcome to the Foundations of Coding Full Stack course.

Here you'll master building and managing every web application layer from dynamic front-end interfaces to powerful back-end systems.

We'll dive into API integration,version control,and security best practices,giving you the tools to create robust,scalable applications.

By the end,you'll have the skills and confidence to bring full stack projects to life and thrive in today's tech-driven world.

We'll start by examining full-stack development,uncovering how it combines front-end and back-end expertise to create seamless applications.

We'll explore the unique role of full-stack developers and the key tools and technologies that make them invaluable in the industry.

Next,we'll strengthen your problem-solving skills,learning to tackle challenges with clear logic and strategic thinking.

We'll explore powerful techniques to break down complex tasks and write efficient code ready for real-world use.

Next,we'll explore client-server architecture,the differences between HTTP and HTTPS,and the principles of RESTful APIs and Graph QL.

We'll also examine how to use Web Sockets for real-time communication,enhancing dynamic application interactions.

Then,we'll explore the basics of databases,exploring relational and non-relational structures.

You'll learn to design and manage database schemas effectively,and then focus on strategies to optimize database performance for maximum efficiency.

Next,we'll cover the essentials of version control,exploring Git and Git Hub for collaboration,and dive into advanced Git techniques.

You will apply best practices to create a conceptual design for a full stack application.

Finally,you will apply coding concepts and problem-solving techniques,creating algorithms and flowcharts,use control structures and loops,and develop modular code leading to a comprehensive coding project.

Throughout this course,you'll master full stack development,from building front-end interfaces to managing back-end systems.

You'll learn to integrate APIs,optimize databases,and use version control,equipping you to create dynamic,scalable applications.

We're excited to guide you in becoming a skilled full-stack developer.

01-01.03_讲师介绍

我是保罗·帕蒂，这门课程的讲师。

我从十几岁起就开始编程了。

我热爱编程，因为它就像是在设计并创造出惊人作品的同时，解开一个个谜题。

我的研究生学位是哲学，在那里我学会了如何成为更好的思考者。

当我编写计算机程序时，我可以运用许多在哲学中学到的逻辑技巧。

计算机编程不仅能让你构建很酷的软件，而且我相信它也能帮助你成为更好的思考者。

我在微软开始了我的软件开发职业生涯，那时互联网才刚刚起步，我亲身参与了互联网革命。

我管理的软件工程团队专注于桌面端和基于网页的软件。

互联网为软件开发人员，尤其是那些从事用户界面开发的，带来了难以置信的新机遇。

从移动界面到网站，软件开发人员不仅在创造功能性的软件，也在创造艺术作品。

在我的职业生涯中，我在微软和Pay Pal管理并参与了多个专注于面向客户界面的团队，并学习了如何将它们做得更好。

在微软工作的二十年里，我有一部分时间在培训部门度过，在那里我学会了如何向新学员传达具有挑战性的想法。

我还有幸在西雅图的一所大学教授了十五年的非专业哲学课程。

这进一步强化了我使用学员能理解的概念来教授他们复杂难懂知识的能力。

没有什么比亲眼目睹学员茅塞顿开、理解新知识的那一刻更让人有成就感了。

我对这个课程感到非常兴奋。

我相信微软拥有一些最强大、最令人兴奋的软件开发工具，我迫不及待地想让大家了解它们。

我确信，随着你们学习新技能并巩固现有知识，你们将会有很多茅塞顿开的时刻。

英文原文

My name is Paul Party and I'm your instructor for this course.

I've been programming computers since I was in my teens.

I love programming because it's like solving puzzles while designing and creating something amazing.

My graduate degree is in philosophy where I learned how to become a better thinker.

When I program computers,I can apply many of the logic skills I learned in philosophy.

Computer programming not only enables you to build cool software,but I believe it can help make you a better thinker.

I started my software development career at Microsoft when the internet was just getting started,and I participated in the internet revolution firsthand.

I managed software engineering teams that worked on desktop and web-based software.

The internet gave software developers,particularly those working on user interface,incredible new opportunities.

From mobile interfaces to websites,software developers are not only creating software that's functional,but also works of art.

In my career,I managed and worked with teams at both Microsoft and Pay Pal that focused on customer-facing interfaces and learned how to make them great.

I also spent part of my 20 years at Microsoft in training organizations where I learned how to communicate challenging ideas to new learners.

I also was fortunate enough to have spent 15 years teaching non-majors philosophy at a university in Seattle.

This reinforced my ability to teach learners difficult concepts using ideas they can relate to.

There's nothing more satisfying than to witness a learner having an aha moment where they understand something new.

I'm really excited about this program.

I believe Microsoft has some of the most powerful and exciting software development tools available,and I can't wait for you to learn about them.

I'm confident you'll have many of the aha moments as you gain new skills and reinforce existing ones.

01-01.05_课程内容详解

本课程的学习体验旨在通过各种学习方法，让您全面理解关键概念。

我们将引导您学习互动式、引人入胜的材料，帮助您建立实践和理论知识。

无论您对前端、后端还是全栈开发感兴趣，本课程都提供了支持您每一步成长的工具。

在整个课程中，您将通过视频了解核心概念和关键思想。

这些视频旨在帮助您清晰、简洁地理解定义、过程和基本步骤。

您将吸收基础知识，为后续更深入的探索奠定基础。

当您需要逐步指导时，录屏教程将为您提供详细的实践视角。

这些录屏教程将精确地向您展示屏幕上的内容，以及如何完成特定的编码任务。

本课程还包括阅读材料，以强化视频中提出的关键思想。

这些阅读材料不仅仅是补充材料，它们是工作辅助工具。

您可以打印出来，在完成作业或活动时用作参考，从而更轻松地将所学知识应用于实际情境。

课程中包含了小型活动，帮助您检验对关键思想的理解。

01-01.11_项目介绍

每一个成功的编码项目都始于一个周密的计划。

通过仔细地组织和规划您的项目，您将确保所有部分能够顺利地整合在一起。

这个过程看似是额外的工作，但它为成功奠定了基础。

在这个视频中，我们将解释课程结束时的项目。

这个项目模拟了现实世界的软件开发过程。

您将从设计和规划一个应用程序开始，然后构建基础功能，随着代码变得复杂，进行调试和优化。

最后，您将把所有内容整合成一个完善的最终产品。

目标是体验完整的编码过程：编写、修复和改进您的代码。

在编写任何代码之前，您将规划和设计一个应用程序。这将包括定义项目需求和目标，并创建设计大纲。

计划确定后，您将编写应用程序的核心功能。

这一步建立了将在后续扩展的基本功能。

一旦核心功能就位，错误自然会出现。

然后您应该专注于调试、识别问题，并确保一切正常运行。

在这个阶段优化您的代码对于流畅的性能至关重要。

随着您的系统变得庞大，手动调试会变得耗时。

在这里，我们将使用微软Copilot来帮助快速发现和修复错误。

Copilot提供AI建议，可以简化调试过程并提高系统性能。

到项目结束时，最终产品将是一个随时可用的完善系统。

在这个项目中，您将经历从规划到构建、调试和优化代码的全过程。

遵循结构化的流程，您将创建一个功能性的系统，它反映了真实的开发周期。

这种体验将教会您精心规划、解决问题以及使用像AI这样的工具来优化工作的价值。

01-02.02_全栈开发

想象一下，你正在构建一个可能塑造在线学习未来的网站。

作为一名全栈开发人员，你不仅在设计学生与之交互的用户界面，还在构建驱动它的后端流程。

全栈开发人员拥有一种独特的能力，能够理解应用程序的每一个方面，从用户体验到数据在幕后如何管理。

在本视频中，我们将定义全栈开发。

并且，我们将通过审视它所遵循的原则，进一步探讨全栈开发。

但在我们探讨全栈开发及其原则之前，我们先花点时间来理解它的基础：前端和后端开发。

这些是全栈开发人员花费大部分时间的核心领域，所以让我们来分解一下。

前端是直接在用户设备上运行的那部分应用程序，无论是浏览器、移动应用还是桌面应用。

这是用户与应用程序进行交互的地方。

这种交互包括他们体验和点击的一切：布局、文本、图像、按钮和表单。

另一方面，后端是在服务器上运行的那部分应用程序。

这是奇迹在幕后发生的地方，管理数据库、处理请求，并确保一切功能顺畅。

例如，当你在网站上提交一个表单时，后端处理你的输入，并从服务器检索必要的数据以显示在前端。

现在，我们已经通过定义前端和后端打下了基础，我们可以探讨全栈开发人员如何整合这些领域。

这种整合使得全栈开发人员能够监督和优化整个应用程序，确保面向用户的元素和底层流程协调工作。

全栈开发是指同时开发Web应用程序的前端和后端。

这个角色要求对所涉及的技术以及它们如何协同工作以提供无缝用户体验有广泛的理解。

例如，一个全栈开发人员可能设计网站的界面，同时也编写驱动其功能的服务器端代码。

通过掌握应用程序的两端，全栈开发人员确保系统的所有部分协同工作，提供流畅高效的用户体验。

理解全栈开发让我们得以一窥它的范围。

接下来，我们将探讨使全栈开发人员能够有效管理前端和后端流程的原则。

这些原则确保所有元素，从前端界面到后端流程，都能顺利整合，以提供统一的用户体验。

全栈开发人员应该掌握前端和后端，提供端到端的解决方案，优化性能，优先考虑安全性，确保可访问性，并拥抱响应式设计。

我们将详细讨论这些原则中的每一个，从掌握前端和后端开始。

首先，全栈开发人员需要掌握前端和后端才能有效工作。

这意味着有能力使用HTML、CSS和Java Script等技术构建用户界面，以创建直观的体验。

这也涉及到能够处理服务器端任务，比如使用C#、Python或Ruby等工具进行数据库管理和API集成。

掌握前端和后端使全栈开发人员具备了交付端到端解决方案所需的技能。

全栈开发优先考虑在用户界面和服务器之间创建流畅的交互。

这意味着确保数据从前端到后端，再返回给用户的过程中无缝流动。

例如，一个全栈开发人员可能会专注于确保当用户输入他们的信用卡信息时，这些数据能被快速正确处理，并且用户能立即收到确认信息。

交付端到端解决方案要求全栈开发人员优化性能。

通过微调从用户界面到后端数据库的每一层，全栈开发人员确保应用程序高效运行，为用户提供快速响应的体验。

性能指的是网站或应用程序响应用户请求的速度和效率。

例如，当你在网站上点击一个按钮时，高性能确保页面立即加载，提供流畅的体验。

在全栈开发人员为速度而微调系统时，他们还必须在每一层嵌入强大的安全措施来抵御威胁。

这确保了应用程序始终保持安全和可靠。

安全性指的是开发人员为保护网站和应用程序免受未经授权的访问、数据泄露和其他网络威胁而实施的措施和协议。

作为一名全栈工程师，你必须确保应用程序的每一层，从数据库到用户界面，都受到保护，免受潜在威胁。

正如安全性保护应用程序一样，确保可访问性则能扩大其影响范围。

全栈开发人员必须怀着包容性的心态进行设计，确保他们安全且高性能的应用程序能被所有人使用。

可访问性确保网站和应用程序在各种设备上对所有人都可用，无论其能力如何。

全栈开发人员通过创建能被所有人（包括残障人士）轻松导航和理解的界面，来使网站和应用程序变得可访问。

在可访问性原则的基础上，响应式设计对于确保应用程序能无缝适应各种设备至关重要。

通过拥抱响应式设计，全栈开发人员使他们的应用程序无论在何种屏幕尺寸或方向下，都灵活且用户友好。

响应性指的是网站的设计能够改变其布局和内容以适应不同屏幕尺寸和方向的能力。

响应性确保用户在任何设备上，无论是台式机、笔记本电脑、平板电脑还是智能手机，都能获得易于导航的体验。

在本视频中，我们定义了全栈开发并讨论了它的原则。

通过理解前端和后端开发，并应用这些核心原则，全栈开发人员可以创建统一、高性能且包容性的Web应用程序，满足用户的多样化需求。

英文原文

Imagine you're building a website that could shape the future of online learning.

As a full stack developer,you're not just crafting the user interface that students interact with,you're also engineering the back-end process that powers it.

Full stack developers hold a unique ability to understand every aspect of an application,from what the user experiences to how data is managed behind the scenes.

In this video,we'll define full stack development.

And we'll further explore full stack development by examining the principles it follows.

But before we explore full stack development and its principles,let's take a moment to understand the foundation,front-end and back-end development.

These are the core areas where full stack developers spend most of their time,so let's break them down.

The front-end is the part of the application that runs directly on the user's device,whether it's a browser,mobile app,or desktop app.

It's where the user interacts with the application.

This interaction includes everything they experience and click on,the layout,text,images,buttons,and forms.

On the other hand,the back-end is the part of the application that runs on the server.

It's where the magic happens behind the scenes,managing databases,processing requests,and ensuring that everything functions smoothly.

For instance,when you submit a form on a website,the back end processes your input and retrieves the necessary data from the server to display it on the front end.

Now that we've laid the groundwork by defining front-end and back-end,we can explore how full-stack developers integrate these areas.

This integration allows full-stack developers to supervise and optimize the entire application,ensuring that the user-facing elements and the underlying processes work in harmony.

Full stack development is the development of both the front-end and the back-end of a web application.

The role demands a broad understanding of the technologies involved and how they will work together to deliver a seamless user experience.

For example,a full stack developer might design a website's interface while also writing the server-side code that powers its functionality.

By mastering both sides of the application,full-stack developers ensure that all parts of the system work together,providing a smooth and efficient user experience.

Understanding full stack development gives a glimpse into its scope.

Next,we'll explore the principles that empowers full stack developers to manage both front-end and back-end processes effectively.

These principles ensure that all elements,from front-end interfaces to back-end processes,integrate smoothly to deliver a cohesive user experience.

Full stack developers should master both front-end and back-end,deliver end-to-end solutions,optimize performance,prioritize security,ensure accessibility,and embrace responsive design.

We'll discuss each of these principles in detail,starting with mastering both front-end and back-end.

First,full-stack developers need to master both the front-end and back-end to be effective.

This means having the ability to build user interfaces with technologies like HTML,CSS,and Java Script to create an intuitive experience.

It also involves being able to handle server-side tasks like database management and API integration using tools like C#,Python,or Ruby.

Mastering both the front-end and back-end equips full-stack developers with the skills needed to deliver end-to-end solutions.

Full stack development prioritizes creating smooth interactions between the user interface and the server.

This means ensuring that data flows seamlessly from the front-end to the back end and back to the user.

For example,a full-stack developer might focus on ensuring that when a user inputs their credit card information,that data is correctly processed quickly and the user immediately receives a confirmation message.

Delivering end-to-end solutions requires full stack developers to optimize performance.

By fine-tuning every layer from the user interface to the back-end database,full-stack developers ensure that the application runs efficiently,providing users with a fast and responsive experience.

Performance refers to how quickly and efficiently the site or application responds to users'requests.

For example,when you click on a button on a website,high performance ensures that the page loads instantly,providing a smooth experience.

As full stack developers fine-tune the system for speed,they must also embed robust security measures at every layer to protect against threats.

This ensures that the application remains safe and reliable.

Security refers to the measures and protocols developers implement to protect websites and applications from unauthorized access,data breaches,and other cyber threats.

As a full stack engineer,you must ensure that every layer of your application is protected from the database to the user interface against potential threats.

Just as security safeguards the application,ensuring accessibility broadens its reach.

Full stack developers must design with inclusivity in mind,making sure their secure,high-performing applications are usable by everyone.

Accessibility ensures that websites and applications are usable on a variety of devices by everyone,regardless of their abilities.

Full stack developers make websites and applications accessible by creating interfaces that can be easily navigated and understood by everyone,including people with disabilities.

Building on the principle of accessibility,responsive design is essential to ensure that applications adapt seamlessly to various devices.

By embracing responsive design,full-stack developers make their applications flexible and user-friendly regardless of screen size or orientation.

Responsiveness refers to the ability to use the design.of a website to change its layout and content to fit different screen sizes and orientations.

Responsiveness ensures that users have an easily navigable experience on any device,be it a desktop,laptop,tablet,or smartphone.

In this video,we've defined full stack development and discussed its principles.

By understanding both front-end and back-end development and applying these core principles,full-stack developers can create cohesive,high-performing,and inclusive web applications that meet the diverse needs of users.

01-02.03_全栈开发最佳实践

想象一下，你在一家小型初创公司工作，你的工作是构建一个可能改变人们使用技术方式的Web应用程序。

你需要创建一个流畅、用户友好的前端，并确保后端能够每秒处理数千笔交易而不出任何问题。

在这个快节奏的环境中，掌握全栈开发能够让你交付一个在竞争激烈的技术市场中取得成功的产品。

在本视频中，我们将解释全栈开发的最佳实践。

全栈开发是指同时开发Web应用程序的前端和后端。

为了创建高效且有效的Web应用程序，全栈开发人员遵循最佳实践。

这些最佳实践可以分为两大类：技术技能和开发实践。

首先，我们将探讨每个全栈开发人员都需要的技术技能。

技术技能包括熟练掌握前端和后端技术、熟悉版本控制系统以及了解数据库管理。

让我们更详细地探讨每一项。

首先，全栈开发人员必须熟练掌握前端和后端技术。

熟练掌握意味着你不仅熟悉这些技术，而且每天都能熟练、舒适地使用它们。

前端技术包括HTML、CSS和Java Script。

这些技术是Web的构建块，使你能够创建引人入胜的用户界面。

在后端，关键是服务器端语言，如C#、Python、Ruby或Node.js。

掌握这些语言使你能够构建支持整个应用程序的强大服务器逻辑。

请记住，你不需要了解所有这些语言。

接下来，让我们关注版本控制系统。

想象一下，你正在做一个团队项目，突然需要回退到代码的早期版本。

使用像Git这样的版本控制系统，这变得简单而高效，可以为你节省数小时的潜在返工时间。

版本控制系统是帮助开发人员跟踪和管理代码变更的工具。

这些系统允许多个开发人员同时在同一代码库上工作而不会发生冲突。

另一个关键的技术技能是数据库管理知识。

数据库管理涉及以易于访问、管理和更新的方式组织和存储数据。

你应该熟悉像My SQL这样的SQL数据库，以及像Mongo DB这样的No SQL数据库。

正确的数据库管理确保你的应用程序能够高效且安全地存储和检索数据。

有了坚实的技术基础，现在让我们探讨那些将帮助你构建安全、可维护和高效应用程序的开发实践。

这些包括编写干净、可维护的代码，应用安全最佳实践，调试和优化代码，以及持续学习。

让我们更深入地探讨每一项。

首先，编写干净、可维护的代码至关重要。

干净代码指的是易于阅读、理解和修改的代码，而可维护代码指的是其他人可以轻松更新或扩展的代码。

对于全栈开发人员来说，编写干净、可维护的代码意味着遵循编码标准、编写有意义的注释以及逻辑地组织代码。

干净的代码可以减少错误，并使团队更容易协作，从而确保长期成功。

接下来，让我们关注应用安全最佳实践。

安全最佳实践是帮助保护软件和数据免受未经授权访问、泄露或其他威胁的指南。

作为一名全栈开发人员，这意味着实施数据加密、安全身份验证和输入验证，以保护你的应用程序免受威胁。

现在，让我们讨论调试和优化代码。

调试侧重于识别和修复代码中的错误，而优化则提高代码的效率和性能。

这包括减少加载时间、改进数据库查询以及简化代码。

高效的代码带来更好的性能，这对用户体验至关重要。

最后，让我们谈谈持续学习。

持续学习指的是不断发展新技能和知识的过程。

在快节奏的技术行业，及时了解最新的工具和趋势对于职业发展至关重要。

作为一名全栈开发人员，你应该不断探索新技术、框架和最佳实践，以保持竞争力。

参与持续学习有助于你适应领域内的变化和进步，确保你始终保持一名高效的开发者。

在本视频中，我们探讨了全栈开发的最佳实践。

我们讨论了熟练掌握前端和后端技术、编写干净可维护的代码、熟悉版本控制系统、了解数据库管理、应用安全最佳实践、调试和优化代码以及持续学习的重要性。

通过掌握这些最佳实践，你将做好充分准备来应对全栈开发的挑战。

英文原文

Imagine you're working at a small startup and your job is to build a web application that could change how people use technology.

You need to create a smooth,user-friendly front-end and make sure the back-end can handle thousands of transactions per second without any issues.

In this fast-paced environment,mastering full stack development enables the delivery of a product that can succeed in a competitive tech market.

In this video,we will explain the best practices of full stack development.

Full stack development is the development of both the front end and back end of a web application.

To create efficient and effective web applications,full stack developers follow best practices.

These best practices can be grouped into two main categories,technical skills and development practices.

First,we'll explore the technical skills that every full stack developer needs.

Technical skills include proficiency in front-end and back-end technologies,familiarity with version control systems,and knowledge of database management.

Let's explore each of these in more detail.

First,full-stack developers must be proficient in both front-end and back-end technologies.

Being proficient means you're not just familiar with these technologies,you're skilled and comfortable working with them every day.

Front-end technologies include HTML,CSS,and Java Script.

These technologies are the building blocks of the web and enable you to create engaging user interfaces.

On the back end,it's all about server-side languages like C#,Python,Ruby,or Node.js.

Mastery of these languages allows you to build robust server logic that supports the entire application.

Keep in mind that you do not need to know all of these.

Next,let's focus on version control systems.

Imagine you're working on a team project and suddenly you need to revert to an earlier version of your code.

With version control systems like Git,this is simple and efficient,saving you hours of potential rework.

Version control systems are tools that help developers track and manage changes to their code.

These systems allow multiple developers to work on the same code base simultaneously without conflicts.

Another critical technical skill is knowledge of database management.

Database management involves organizing and storing data in a way that can be easily accessed,managed,and updated.

You should be familiar with SQL databases like My SQL as well as No SQL databases like Mongo DB.

Proper database management ensures that your application can store and retrieve data efficiently and securely.

With a strong technical foundation in place,let's now explore the development practices that will help you build secure,maintainable,and efficient applications.

These include writing clean,maintainable code,applying security best practices,debugging and optimizing code,and continuous learning.

Let's explore each of these in more depth.

First,writing clean,maintainable code is crucial.

Clean code refers to code that is easy to read,understand,and modify,while maintainable code is code that can be easily updated or expanded upon by others.

Full stack developers,writing clean,maintainable code means following coding standards,writing meaningful comments,and organizing your code logically.

Clean code reduces bugs and makes it easier for teams to work together,ensuring long-term success.

Next,let's focus on applying security best practices.

Security best practices are guidelines that help protect software and data from unauthorized access,breaches,or other threats.

As a full-stack developer,this means implementing data encryption,secure authentication,and input validation to protect your applications from threats.

Now,let's discuss debugging and optimizing code.

Debugging focuses on identifying and fixing errors in code while optimization enhances code efficiency and performance.

This includes reducing load times,improving database queries,and simplifying your code.

Efficient code leads to better performance,which is crucial for user experience.

Finally,let's talk about continuous learning.

Continuous learning refers to the ongoing process of developing new skills and knowledge.

In the fast-paced tech industry,staying updated with latest tools and trends is essential for career growth.

As a full-stack developer,you should always be exploring new technologies,frameworks,and best practices to stay competitive.

Engaging in continuous learning helps you adapt to changes and advances in the field,ensuring that you remain an effective developer.

In this video,we explored the best practices of full-stack development.

We discussed the importance of proficiency in front-end and back-end technologies,writing clean and maintainable code,familiarity with version control systems,knowledge of database management,applying security best practices,debugging and optimizing code,and continuous learning.

By mastering these best practices,you'll be well equipped to tackle the challenges of full-stack development.

01-03.01_关键角色与职责

想象一下，你正处在一个团队中，每个人都有自己的专长。

有些人专注于视觉呈现，有些人则专注于幕后的逻辑。

但谁来确保所有这些部分能够无缝衔接呢？

这就是全栈开发人员的用武之地。

全栈开发人员的角色是当今科技行业中最具多面性和最重要的角色之一。

全栈开发人员是技术娴熟的专业人士，他们融合了前端和后端开发，确保应用程序既具有视觉吸引力，又功能强大。

在本视频中，我们将明确全栈开发人员在行业中的关键角色。

我们还将通过审视全栈开发人员的职责，更详细地探讨这个角色。

在我们进一步探索全栈开发人员的世界时，让我们从他们的主要角色开始。

全栈开发人员的主要角色是弥合前端和后端开发之间的差距。

他们理解两端，并确保应用程序的所有组件协同工作。

既然你了解了全栈开发人员的主要角色，让我们探讨一下使全栈开发人员能够履行这一角色的具体职责。

全栈开发人员可以在开发团队中承担几项关键职责，这些职责涉及技术栈的不同部分，以确保应用程序平稳运行。

这些职责包括前端开发、后端开发、数据库管理、安全性、开发运维以及测试和调试或修复代码中的问题。

让我们更详细地审视每一项职责，从前端开发开始。

精心打造的用户界面对于任何应用程序都至关重要，全栈开发人员通过负责前端开发来迎接这一挑战。

前端开发涉及构建和维护一个或多个应用程序的用户界面。

在这个角色中，开发人员使用诸如超文本标记语言（也称为HTML）来实现网站或应用程序的设计和功能，以构建网页内容的结构。

他们使用层叠样式表（也称为CSS）来设计内容的样式，使其具有视觉吸引力。

最后，他们使用Java Script来添加交互式元素，如表单验证或动态内容加载。

例如，全栈开发人员可能会创建一个在移动设备和台式机上都能良好运行的响应式导航栏。

虽然无缝的用户界面很重要，但为前端功能提供基础的是后端系统。

让我们探讨一下全栈开发人员如何确保这两方面协同工作。

这就是后端开发职责的切入点，它专注于驱动应用程序的服务器端基础设施。

在这个角色中，他们管理数据库、服务器和API，以确保后端与前端有效通信。

例如，设置一个允许前端从数据库获取用户数据的API。

为了确保后端平稳运行，全栈开发人员还必须管理应用程序的数据。

这就引出了另一个关键职责：数据库管理。全栈开发人员在此负责设计和管理存储应用程序数据的数据库。

他们确保数据被安全存储，并且应用程序能够高效地检索数据。

例如，设计一个数据库来安全存储用户信息，并在需要用户身份验证时检索这些信息。

数据就位后，下一个关键重点是保护整个系统。

当全栈开发人员承担起保护数据和维护用户信任的职责时，安全性变得至关重要。

在这个角色中，他们实施安全措施，如加密、安全身份验证和授权，以保护应用程序。

例如，设置用户身份验证并加密敏感用户数据，以防止未经授权的访问。

安全问题得到处理后，下一个主要挑战是通过部署将应用程序变为现实。

在此，全栈开发人员可以承担开发运维的职责，负责将应用程序部署到生产环境。

这涉及设置CI CD流水线，以自动化将应用程序部署到服务器的过程。

例如，使用Docker将应用程序容器化，并将其部署到云服务器。

即使在部署之后，工作也远未结束。

确保应用程序完美运行需要持续的测试和调试。

全栈开发人员作为问题解决者，承担着识别和修复前端和后端问题的关键任务。

例如，调试一个由于后端逻辑错误而导致前端功能无法正确显示的问题。

每一项职责都层层递进，帮助全栈开发人员履行他们连接前端和后端开发的主要角色。

在本视频中，你了解了全栈开发人员在行业中的关键角色和职责。

他们能够同时处理前端和后端任务的能力，使得他们在确保应用程序无缝集成和功能完善方面不可或缺。

理解这一角色及其职责，可以帮助你认识到全栈开发人员在技术领域中的多面性和重要性。

英文原文

Imagine you're working on a team where everyone has a specialized role.

Some focus on the visuals,other on the behind-the-scenes logic.

But who ensures that all these pieces fit together?

That's where the full stack developer comes in.

The role of a full stack developer is one of the most versatile and essential in the tech industry today.

Full stack developers are skilled professionals who bring together front-end and back-end development,ensuring the applications are both visually appealing and functionally robust.

In this video,we will identify the key roles of full-stack developers in the industry.

We'll also explore that role in more detail by examining the responsibilities full-stack developers have.

As we further explore the full stack developers'world,let's start with our primary role.

The primary role of a full-stack developer is to bridge the gap between the front-end and back-end development.

They understand both sides and ensure all components of an application work together.

Now that you understand the primary role of a full-stack developer,let's explore specific responsibilities that enable full-stack developers to fulfill this role.

Full stack developers can take on several key responsibilities within a development team,which involve working across different parts of the technology stack to ensure the application runs smoothly.

These responsibilities include front-end development,back-end development,database management,security,Dev Ops,and testing and debugging or repairing issues in the code.

Let's examine each of these responsibilities in more detail,starting with front-end development.

A well-crafted user interface is crucial for any application,and full-stack developers take on this challenge by being responsible for front-end development.

Front-end development involves building and maintaining the user interface of one or more applications.

In this role,developers implement the designs and functionalities of a website or application using languages such as hypertext markup language,also known as HTML,to structure the content of a web page.

They use cascading style sheets,also known as CSS,to style that content,making it visually appealing.

Finally,they use Java Script to add interactive elements like form validation or dynamic content loading.

For example,full stack developers might create a responsive navigation bar that works well on both mobile devices and desktops.

While a seamless user interface is important,it's the back-end systems that provide the foundation for front-end functionality.

Let's explore how full-stack developers ensure that these two sides work together.

This is where the responsibility of backend development comes in,focusing on the server-side infrastructure that powers the application.

In this role,they manage databases,servers,and APIs to ensure that the back-end communicates effectively with the front-end.

For example,setting up an API that allows The front-end to get user data from the database.

To ensure that the back-end operates smoothly,full-stack developers must also manage the application's data.

This leads to another crucial responsibility,database management,where full-stack developers design and manage the databases that store the application's data.

They ensure that data is stored securely and can be retrieved efficiently by the application.

For instance,designing a database to store user information securely and retrieving it when needed for user authentication.

With data in place,the next critical focus is on safeguarding the entire system.

Security becomes crucial when full-stack developers take on the responsibility of protecting data and maintaining user trust.

In this role,they implement security measures such as encryption,secure authentication,and authorization to safeguard the application.

For example,setting up user authentication and encrypting sensitive user data to protect unauthorized access.

With security in check,the next major challenge is bringing the application to life through deployment.

Here,full-stack developers can take on Dev Ops responsibilities where they handle the deployment of applications to production environments.

This involves setting up CI CD pipelines to automate application deployments to a server.

For example,using Docker to containerize an application and deploying it to a cloud server.

Even after deployment,the job is far from over.

Ensuring that applications run flawlessly requires continuous testing and debugging.

Full stack developers,as problem solvers,take on the critical task of identifying and fixing issues across both the front-end and the back-end.

For instance,debugging an issue where a front-end feature is not displaying correctly due to an error in the back-end logic.

Each responsibility builds on the next to help full-stack developers fulfill their primary role of connecting front-end and back-end development.

In this video,you learned about the key roles and responsibilities of full-stack developers in the industry.

Their ability to work on both front-end and back-end tasks make them invaluable in ensuring the seamless integration and functionality of applications.

Understanding this role and its responsibilities can help you appreciate the versatility and importance of full-stack developers in the tech world.

01-03.02_行业需求与职业机会

作为一名全栈开发人员，你的工作塑造着数字世界，从创建用户友好的界面，到为其背后的引擎提供动力。

在当今技术驱动的格局中，你的多面技能至关重要，无论是对于初创公司的应用还是大型企业的系统而言。

你的角色比以往任何时候都更重要、更令人兴奋。

在本视频中，我们将介绍行业对全栈开发人员的需求以及潜在的职业发展路径。

全栈开发人员具备构建和维护Web应用程序各个方面的技能，从前端（用户理解和交互的部分）到后端（处理逻辑、数据和安全的层面）。

无论你对构建动态网站、开发强大的应用程序，还是甚至设计软件系统架构感兴趣，都有一条适合你的道路。

我们将介绍的角色包括：全栈开发人员、全栈应用程序开发人员、软件架构师和开发运维工程师。

了解这些角色中的每一个，都将让你更清晰地看到自己潜在的职业道路。

我们将从探索全栈Web开发人员的角色开始。

作为全栈Web开发人员，你的重点是创建和维护网站，确保其功能完善、美观，并优化性能。

但如果你对开发超越网站之外的软件更感兴趣呢？

那么全栈应用程序开发人员的角色可能适合你。

全栈应用程序开发人员负责构建和维护可在台式计算机、移动设备或网络平台上使用的软件应用程序，让你有机会在不同媒介上工作，并覆盖更广泛的受众。

随着你在开发网站或应用程序方面积累经验，你可能会自然而然地朝着更具战略性的职位发展。

这就引出了软件架构师的角色。

作为软件架构师，你的重点从构建单个组件转移到设计应用程序的整体结构，确保它们无缝协作。

全栈开发人员非常适合这个职位，因为他们对前端和后端技术的深刻理解，使他们能够就应用程序的每个部分如何融入整体架构做出明智的决策。

另一方面，如果你发现自己对软件的部署和运维方面更感兴趣，你可能会考虑开发运维工程师的角色。

开发运维工程师管理应用程序的部署、基础设施和维护。

全栈开发人员非常适合开发运维工作，因为他们在整个应用程序生命周期（从开发到生产）的广泛专业知识，使他们能够专注于确保系统顺利部署和可靠运行。

许多不同的行业，包括科技、金融、医疗保健、电子商务和娱乐业，都需要全栈开发人员。

你创建完整数字产品的能力使你成为宝贵的资产。

在这个行业，你可能正在为公司开发内部工具，或者为用户开发最终产品。

例如，金融机构需要强大且安全的应用程序，用于从在线银行到投资管理的各个方面。

全栈开发人员在构建和维护这些系统方面发挥着关键作用。

而医疗保健行业的全栈开发人员则为患者管理、远程医疗和数据分析构建数字解决方案。

类似地，在线零售商需要全栈开发人员来构建和维护他们的网站、管理库存系统，并为客户提供无缝的购物体验。

全栈开发人员在娱乐行业也至关重要。

从流媒体服务到游戏平台，都需要全栈开发人员来创建引人入胜且可扩展的数字体验。

有了这些了解，现在让我们探讨全栈开发人员可以获得的特定职业机会。

在自由职业、在初创公司工作或加入大型企业之间做出选择，会显著影响你的职业体验。

这些环境在团队规模、工作节奏以及你所承担的责任级别方面差异很大。

让我们从自由职业开始，更详细地探讨这些机会。

许多全栈开发人员选择作为自由职业者或顾问工作，向各种客户提供他们多方面的技能。

作为自由职业者，你会有大量的责任，但在你从事的项目类型方面拥有很大的灵活性。

例如，一个自由职业的全栈开发人员可以为不同的公司承担特定的游戏应用程序项目，以合同形式提供全面的开发服务。

如果自己创业对你没有吸引力，但你想体验许多不同的职责，那么你可能喜欢在初创公司工作。

全栈开发人员在初创公司中因其处理多项职责的能力而备受重视。

初创公司通常节奏很快，员工会从事许多不同的任务。

例如，一家初创公司可能需要一个全栈开发人员从头开始构建新产品、管理实施新的营销工具，或保护公司的云服务器。

这种多样化的任务范围可能非常有成就感，但如果你更喜欢结构化的环境，并有机会在大型团队中工作，那么你可能考虑在公司工作。

大型科技公司通常寻求全栈开发人员来构建他们的产品，并维护和扩展他们的平台。

当你考虑未来可能想要扮演的角色时，了解全栈开发人员可以受聘的角色类型、工作类型和行业是很重要的。

As a full stack developer,your work shapes the digital world from creating user-friendly interfaces to powering the engines behind them.

In today's tech-driven landscape,your versatile skills are crucial,whether it's for a startup app or a corporation's systems.

Your role is more important and exciting than ever.

In this video,we will describe the industry demand for full-stack developers and potential career paths.

A full-stack developer has the skills to build and maintain every aspect of a web application from the front end,which users understand and interact with,to the back end,which processes logic,data,and security.

Whether you're interested in building dynamic websites,developing powerful applications,or even shaping the architecture of software systems,there's a path for you.

The roles we'll cover include full-stack developer,full-stack application developer,software architect,and Dev Ops engineer.

Understanding each of these roles will give you a clearer picture of your potential career path.

We'll start by exploring the role of a full-stack web developer.

As a full-stack web developer,your focus is on creating and maintaining websites,ensuring they are functional and aesthetically appealing,and optimizing performance.

But what if you're more interested in developing software beyond just websites?

The role of a full-stack application developer might be for you.

A full-stack application developer is responsible for building and maintaining software applications that can be used on desktop computers,mobile devices,or web platforms,giving you the opportunity to work across different mediums and reach a wider audience.

As you gain experience developing websites or applications,you might naturally progress towards a more strategic position.

This brings us to the role of software architect.

As a software architect,your focus shifts from building individual components to designing the overall structure of applications,ensuring they work seamlessly.

Full stack developers are well suited for this position because their deep understanding of both front-end and back-end technologies equips them to make informed decisions about how each piece of the application fits into the larger picture.

On the other hand,if you find yourself drawn to the deployment and operations side of software,you might consider the role of a Dev Ops engineer.

Dev Ops engineers manage application deployment,infrastructure,and maintenance.

Full stack developers are well suited for Dev Ops because their broad expertise across the application lifecycle,from development to production,enables them to specialize in ensuring that systems are deployed smoothly and operate reliably.

Many different industries,including technology,finance,healthcare,and e-commerce,and entertainment,need full-stack developers.

Let's explore how these industries need full-stack developers starting with the technology industry.

From startups to large corporations,tech companies are major employers of full-stack developers.

Your ability to create complete digital products makes you a valuable asset.

In this industry,you might be working on an internal tool for the company or an end product for users.

Financial institutions,for example,require robust and secure applications for everything from online banking to investment management.

Full-stack developers play a key role in building and maintaining these systems.

And full-stack developers in the healthcare industry build digital solutions for patient management,telemedicine,and data analysis.

In a similar way,online retailers need full-stack developers to build and maintain their websites,manage inventory systems,and provide a seamless shopping experience for customers.

Full-stack developers are also vital in the entertainment industry.

From streaming services to gaming platforms,full-stack developers are needed to create engaging and scalable digital experiences.

With these insights in mind,let's now explore the specific career opportunities available to full-stack developers.

The choice between freelancing,working at a startup,or joining a large corporation can significantly impact your experience.

These environments differ greatly in terms of team size,the speed of work,and the level of responsibilities you shoulder.

Let's explore these opportunities in more detail,starting with freelancing.

Many full-stack developers choose to work as freelancers or consultants,offering their versatile skills to a range of clients.

As a freelancer,you'll have a large number of responsibilities,but have a lot of flexibility in terms of the types of projects you work on.

For example,a freelance full-stack developer could take on specific gaming application projects for different companies,providing comprehensive development services on a contract basis.

If running your own business doesn't seem attractive,but you want to experience many different responsibilities,you might enjoy working at a startup.

Full-stack developers are highly valued in startups due to their ability to handle multiple responsibilities.

Startups are often fast-paced and employ work on a number of different tasks.

For example,a startup might need a full-stack developer to build a new product from scratch,manage implementing a new marketing tool,or secure the company's cloud servers.

This diverse range of tasks can be incredibly rewarding,but if you prefer a more structured environment with the opportunity to work on a large team,you might consider working at a corporation.

Large tech companies often seek full-stack developers to build their products and maintain and expand their platforms.

Understanding the types of roles,job types,and industries full-stack developers can get hired into is important as you consider the role you might want in the future.

01-04.01_前端 vs 后端开发

回想一下你最近使用的网站或应用。它美观吗？

加载速度快吗？

这些体验是前端和后端良好协调的结果。但这些角色具体涉及什么，它们又是如何协同工作，创造出我们每天依赖的数字产品的呢？

全栈开发人员弥合了前端和后端开发之间的鸿沟，确保用户界面既美观又实用，同时底层系统也能平稳运行。

理解两端对于交付完整、用户友好的体验至关重要。

在本视频中，我们将比较前端和后端开发人员的角色与职责。首先，让我们探讨一下为什么这种比较对全栈开发人员有帮助。

作为一名全栈开发人员，你需要处理来自两端的工作任务，因此比较它们有助于你把握角色的全貌。

通过比较，你会更清晰地了解每一端涉及的内容，从而帮助你平衡工作并找出需要改进的领域。

这种比较也能增强协作能力。了解它们的区别，让你能更好地与前端和后端的团队成员沟通，从而促成更顺畅的团队合作。

让我们来定义一下前端开发和后端开发。

前端开发是构建用户直接与之交互的网站或Web应用的视觉和交互元素的实践。

但是用户不与之交互的那部分应用程序呢？

这就是后端开发的用武之地。后端开发是构建和管理驱动应用和网站的系统（如数据处理、存储和应用逻辑）的实践。

鉴于这些差异，前端和后端工程师的职责是什么？

我们先从前端和后端开发人员共同的职责开始。前端、后端和全栈开发人员每天都要处理三项重要任务：编码、测试和调试。

尽管前端和后端开发人员每天都会花大量时间在编码上，但他们的关注点和使用技术是不同的。

当我们谈论编码时，指的是使用编程语言来实现网站或应用的前端设计、功能以及后端系统。

例如，前端开发人员使用诸如超文本标记语言（或称HTML）来构建网页内容的结构，以实现应用的视觉和交互元素，使用层叠样式表（或称CSS）来设计内容的样式，使其具有视觉吸引力，并使用Java Script来添加交互式元素，如表单验证或动态内容加载。

而大多数后端工程师每天会花大量时间编写服务器端代码。

编写服务器端代码涉及创建驱动应用程序的逻辑。后端工程师使用诸如C#、Java Script、Ruby和Python等语言来编写服务器端代码。

完成编码阶段后，下一个关键步骤是测试。前端和后端开发人员在此阶段都要确保代码功能正确、与应用程序的其他部分流畅交互，并符合用户体验标准。

开发人员会进行不同类型的测试，例如针对特定代码段的单元测试、确保各部分协同工作的集成测试，以及确认网站在实际条件下行为正确的用户界面测试。测试之后，开发人员会花时间进行调试。

这意味着查找并修复在测试过程中发现的错误或问题，以确保一切正常运行。

除了这些共同的日常任务，前端开发人员和后端开发人员还有各自特定的职责。前端开发人员专注于响应性、可访问性和安全性，而后端开发人员则承担诸如API开发、数据管理和系统可靠性等职责。

我们来详细了解一下这些职责，从前端开发人员的响应性职责开始。响应性指的是网站改变其布局和内容以适应不同屏幕尺寸和方向的能力。

响应性确保用户在任何设备上，无论是台式机、笔记本电脑、平板电脑还是智能手机，都能获得易于导航的体验。

既然我们已经探讨了前端开发人员如何确保网站适应不同设备，让我们再考虑另一个重要职责：可访问性。

可访问性确保网站和应用程序对所有人（无论其能力如何）都是可用的。前端开发人员通过创建能被所有人（包括残障人士）轻松导航和理解的界面，来使网站和应用程序变得可访问。

与响应性和可访问性使各类用户都能使用网站和应用程序类似，前端开发人员还负责安全性，确保这些用户能够安全地在线执行日益增多和多样化的任务。

安全性指的是开发人员为保护网站和应用程序免受未经授权的访问、数据泄露和其他网络威胁而实施的措施和协议。

现在让我们探讨后端开发人员的一些主要职责，从API开发开始。

首先，后端工程师负责API开发，包括创建、文档编写和维护API。应用程序编程接口，简称API，是一套允许不同软件应用程序之间相互通信的规则和工具。

后端开发人员通过确保API高效、安全且文档齐全，来实现各种系统间的无缝数据交换和功能。接下来，后端开发人员还负责数据管理和安全。

数据是由计算机收集、存储和使用的信息，通常以文本、数字或其他格式存在，用于驱动各种应用程序中的决策和操作。

数据管理和安全涉及管理数据流、高效存储数据以及保护敏感数据。

最后，后端工程师负责系统可靠性和维护。

这包括强大的错误处理、日志记录、定期维护和更新。这些实践有助于后端工程师快速识别和解决问题，最大限度地减少停机时间，确保稳定的用户体验，并使系统保持安全并处于最佳运行状态。

尽管他们的任务和职责不同，但前端和后端开发人员都为构建强大、用户友好的应用程序做出了贡献。

在本视频中，我们比较了前端和后端开发人员的角色与职责。每个角色对开发过程都至关重要，他们的协作努力确保了创建出功能完善、用户友好且健壮的应用程序。

作为一名全栈开发人员，理解这些角色不仅使你更具多面性，也使你能够创建更统一、更高性能的应用程序。

英文原文

Think about the last website or app you used.Was it beautiful?

Did it load quickly?

These experiences are the result of a well-coordinated front-end and back-end.But what exactly do these roles involve and how do they work together to create the digital products we rely on every day?

Full stack developers bridge the gap between front-end and back-end development,ensuring that the user interface is both beautiful and functional,while the underlying systems work smoothly.

Understanding both sides is crucial for delivering a complete,user-friendly experience.

In this video,we will compare the roles and responsibilities of front-end and back-end developers.First,let's explore why this comparison is helpful for full-stack developers.

As a full-stack developer,you're expected to handle tasks from both sides,so comparing them helps you grasp the full scope of your role.

By comparing them,you'll get a clearer picture of what each side involves,helping you to balance your work and identify areas to improve.

This comparison also enhances collaboration.Knowing the distinctions allows you to communicate better with front-end and back-end team members,leading to smoother teamwork.

Let's define both front-end and back-end development.

Front-end development is the practice of building the visual and interactive elements of a website or web application that users directly interact with.

But what about the parts of the application that users don't interact with?

That's where back-end development comes in.Backend development is the practice of building and managing the systems that power apps and websites,such as data processing,storage,and application logic.

Given these differences,what are the responsibilities of front-end and back-end engineers?

Let's start with the responsibilities that front-end engineers and back-end developers have in common.Front-end,back-end,and full-stack developers spend their days on three important tasks,coding,testing,and debugging.

Even though both front-end developers and back-end developers spend a large portion of the day coding,their focus and the technologies they use are different.

When we talk about coding,we are referring to implementing the front-end designs,functionalities,and back-end systems of a website or application using programming languages.

For example,front-end developers implement an application's visual and interactive elements using languages such as Hypertext Markup Language or HTML to structure a web page's content,cascading style sheets or CSS to style that content,making it visually appealing,and Java Script to add interactive elements like form validation or dynamic content loading.

And most backend engineers spend a significant portion of their day writing server-side code.

Writing server-side code involves creating the logic that powers the application.Backend engineers write server-side code in languages.like C#,Java Script,Ruby,and Python.

After completing the coding phase,the next crucial step is testing where both front-end and back-end developers ensure the code functions correctly,interacts smoothly with other parts of the application,and meets user experience standards.

Developers conduct different types of tests such as unit tests for specific sections of code,integration tests to ensure parts work together,and user interface tests to confirm their sites behave correctly in real-world conditions.After testing,developers spend time debugging.

This means finding and fixing the bugs or issues discovered during testing to make sure everything works correctly.

Along with these common daily tasks,front-end developers and back-end developers have specific responsibilities.While front-end developers focus on responsiveness,accessibility,and security,backend developers take on responsibilities such as API development,data management,and system reliability.

Let's go into more detail about each of these responsibilities,starting with the front-end responsibility of responsiveness.Responsiveness refers to the ability of a website to change its layout and content to fit different screen sizes and orientations.

Responsiveness ensures that users have an easily navigable experience on any device,be it a desktop,laptop,tablet,or smartphone.

Now that we've explored how front-end developers ensure websites adapt to different devices,let's consider another important responsibility,accessibility.

Accessibility ensures that websites and applications are usable by everyone regardless of their abilities.Front-end developers make websites and applications accessible by creating interfaces that can be easily navigated and understood by everyone,including people with disabilities.

Similar to how responsiveness and accessibility enable a diverse range of users to use websites and applications,front-end developers are responsible for security,which ensures these users can safely perform a growing and diverse range of tasks online.

Security refers to the measures and protocols developers implement to protect websites and applications from unauthorized access,data breaches,and other cyber threats.

Now let's explore some of the back-end developers'primary responsibilities,starting with API development.

First,backend engineers are responsible for API development,which includes creating,documenting,and maintaining APIs.An application programming interface,or API for short,is a set of rules and tools that allow different software applications to communicate with each other.

Backend developers enable seamless data exchange and functionality across various systems by ensuring that APIs are efficient,secure,and well-documented.Next,back-end developers are also responsible for data management and security.

Data is information that is collected,stored,and used by computers,often in the form of text,numbers,or other formats to drive decisions and operations in various applications.

Data management and security involves managing the flow of data,storing data efficiently,and protecting sensitive data.

Finally,back-end engineers are responsible for system reliability and maintenance.

This includes robust error handling,logging,regular maintenance,and updates.These practices help backend engineers quickly identify and address issues,minimize downtime,ensure a stable user experience,and keep the system secure and functioning at its best.

While their tasks and responsibilities differ,both front-end and back-end developers contribute to robust user-friendly applications.

In this video,we compare the roles and responsibilities of front-end and back-end developers.Each role is vital to the development process,and their collaborative efforts ensure the creation of functional,user-friendly,and robust applications.

As a full-stack developer,understanding these roles not only makes you more versatile,but also equips you to create more cohesive and high-performing applications.

01-04.02_前后端开发整合

你有没有想过，网站和应用程序是如何运行得如此顺畅的？

用户界面的操作方式，以及在后台执行的流程，它们之间相互连接的方式，可能你都没有意识到。

这种连接正是全栈开发的核心所在。

在这个视频中，我们将解释在全栈开发中，前端开发和后端开发是如何整合的。

全栈开发，指的是同时开发一个网络应用程序的前端和后端。

全栈开发人员具备同时处理前端和后端工作的技能，确保用户界面（简称UI）与底层系统无缝连接。

这始于前端开发，其重点是创建响应迅速且交互性强的用户体验。

在构建了稳固且用户友好的前端之后，焦点就转移到幕后的后端，全栈开发人员在此管理驱动整个应用程序的核心功能。

虽然前端开发和后端开发处理应用程序的不同方面，但它们需要无缝地连接起来。

这时，整合就发挥了作用。

大多数全栈开发人员擅长将前端与后端的API和服务进行整合，确保用户界面与底层逻辑无缝协作。

要理解这种整合是如何发生的，让我们更详细地探讨一下API和服务。

应用程序编程接口，简称API，是一套规则和工具，允许不同的软件应用程序之间相互通信。

全栈开发人员不仅要开发API，还要确保它们被有效地整合到整个应用程序架构中。

这些API通常与服务协同工作，服务是服务器端的函数，负责处理特定任务，比如处理请求、管理数据，或在应用程序内处理身份验证。

通过定义数据架构和建立通信协议，全栈开发人员确保前端和后端能够顺畅地协同工作，从而创建一个连贯且功能正常的应用程序。

明确了这些组成部分之后，现在让我们来探讨一下全栈开发人员是如何通过典型的开发工作流将所有部分整合起来的。

要构建一个应用程序，全栈开发人员通常从规划和设计开始，接着是开发，然后是持续整合和测试，最后是部署。

一个成功的开发项目，离不开前期的规划和设计。

在此阶段，开发人员收集需求，与利益相关者协作，并确定技术规范。

一旦设计完成，他们就专注于前端开发，使用HTML、CSS和Java Script框架等工具来构建用户界面。

与此同时，他们也在进行后端工作，搭建服务器、创建数据库并实现应用程序的逻辑。

当前端和后端都开发完成后，重点就转移到整合上。

开发人员将前端与后端的API和服务连接起来，确保数据流顺畅并进行错误处理。

当所有连接都就绪后，就可以开始测试了。

在这个阶段，开发人员会进行全面的测试，以发现并修复任何问题。

一旦所有功能都完美运行，应用程序就会被部署，并且开发人员会持续监控其性能，根据需要进行更新。

在整个工作流程中，全栈开发人员始终专注于整合前端和后端，以创建连贯且功能正常的应用程序。

通过理解并同时处理这两个领域，全栈开发人员确保所有部分协同工作，为用户提供流畅且引人入胜的体验。

在这个视频中，我们解释了在全栈开发中前端开发和后端开发的整合。

我们涵盖了前端开发和后端开发，以及全栈开发人员如何将这两者整合起来。

我们还探讨了全栈开发人员用于整合前端和后端开发，以创建连贯应用程序的工作流程。

英文原文

Have you ever wondered how websites and apps work so seamlessly?

The way the user interface operates and the processes being executed in the background are interconnected in ways you might not even realize.

This connection is at the heart of full stack development.

In this video,we'll explain the integration of front-end and back-end development in full stack development.

Full stack development is the development of both the front-end and back-end of a web application.

Full stack developers have the skills to work across both the front-end and the back-end,ensuring that the user interface referred to as the UI and the underlying systems are seamlessly connected.

This begins with front-end development,which focuses on creating responsive and interactive user experiences.

After establishing a solid and user-friendly front-end,the focus shifts behind the scenes to the back end,where full stack developers manage the core functionality that powers the entire application.

While front-end and back-end development addresses different aspects of the application,they need to be seamlessly connected.

This is where integration comes into play.

Most full-stack developers excel at integrating the front-end with the back-end APIs and services,ensuring that the user interface works seamlessly with the underlying logic.

To understand how this integration happens,let's explore APIs and services in more detail.

An application programming interface,or API for short,is a set of rules and tools that allows different software applications to communicate with each other.

Full stack developers not only develop APIs,but also ensure they are effectively integrated into the overall application architecture.

These APIs often work in conjunction with services,which are server-side functions that handle specific tasks like processing requests,managing data,or handling authentication within an application.

By defining the data architecture and establishing communication protocols,Full Stack developers ensure that the front-end and back-end work together smoothly,creating a cohesive and functional application.

With these components defined,let's now explore how full stack developers bring everything together through a typical development workflow.

To build an application,full-stack developers typically begin with planning and design,next development,then continued integration and testing,and finally deployment.

A successful development project can't begin without planning and design.

Here,the developer gathers requirements,collaborates with stakeholders,and defines the technical specifications.

Once the design is in place,they focus on front-end development using tools like HTML,CSS,and Java Script frameworks to build the user interface.

Simultaneously,they work on the back-end setting up the server,creating the database,and implementing The application's logic.

After both the front-end and back-end are developed,the focus shifts to integration.

The developer connects the front-end with the back-end APIs and services,ensuring smooth data flow and error handling.

Once all the connections are in place,testing can begin.

During this stage,the developer conducts thorough tests to identify and fix any issues.

Once everything is working perfectly,the application is deployed and the developer continues to monitor its performance,making updates as necessary.

Throughout this workflow,Full Stack developers focus on integrating the front-end and back-end to create cohesive functional applications.

By understanding and working across both areas,Full Stack developers ensure that everything works together,providing users with a smooth and engaging experience.

In this video,we explained the integration of front-end and back-end development in full-stack development.

We covered both front-end and back-end development and how full-stack developers integrate the two.

We also explored the workflow full-stack developers use to integrate front-end and back-end development to create cohesive applications.

01-05.01_基本前端技术

在数字世界中，前端技术帮助开发者构建视觉上吸引人且引人入胜的网站和应用程序，确保它们易于访问、用户友好且运行流畅。

在本视频中，我们将列举全栈开发中使用的关键前端技术。

全栈开发中使用的前端技术包括编码语言、框架和库。

首先，让我们从定义开始。

编码语言是开发者用来向计算机发出指令的工具。

理解计算机只能理解二进制代码（即一系列的一和零）这一点很重要。

大多数编码语言的设计方式，既让人类更容易编写，也让机器更容易解释。

通过这种方式，计算机接收你编写的代码，并将其翻译成它能理解的东西，即二进制代码。

既然我们了解了什么是编码语言以及它们如何工作，现在让我们深入探讨前端开发的基石语言：HTML、CSS和Java Script。

让我们从HTML开始，更详细地探讨这些语言。

HTML是用于创建网页的标准标记语言。

它提供了将所有内容整合在一起的所有结构。

无论是文本、图像还是交互功能，HTML都能组织元素，并向你的浏览器提供关于如何显示它们的特定指令。

元素是网页的组成部分，例如段落、链接和媒体文件。

HTML使用标签系统来定义这些元素。

那么这在实践中是如何工作的呢？

HTML中的每个元素都被包裹在标记内容开始和结束的标签中。

一个元素通常由一个开始标签、内容和结束标签组成。

例如，要在HTML中创建一个段落，你可以这样写：左尖括号p右尖括号这是一个段落。

左尖括号斜杠p右尖括号p标签告诉浏览器，其中的文本应该被视为一个段落，而闭合的p标签表示该段落已结束。

现在，虽然有些元素像这个例子一样简单明了，但其他元素带有称为属性的附加选项，这些属性提供额外的细节或设置。

属性通常用于指定诸如图像的来源、按钮的链接或元素的样式等内容。

让我们通过一个例子来探讨属性如何在链接元素中工作。

一个链接元素以开始标签A开头。

这个标签代表anchor，用于创建超链接。

接下来，我们添加一个属性。

在这个例子中，href属性指定了点击链接时将跳转到的URL。

例如，href https www.example.com。

属性后面是用户将要点击的内容。

在这个例子中，是“visit example website”。

最后，我们用结束标签斜杠a来闭合链接。

这标志着链接元素的结束。

现在你知道了如何向网页添加元素，但如果你想改变它们的大小、字体或颜色呢？

HTML的style属性可以修改字体样式和颜色，但对于大型网站来说效率很低。

你需要逐个为每个元素设置样式，这使得更新变得重复且耗时。

这时CSS就派上用场了。

CSS将内容与设计分离，允许你一致地设置多个元素的样式，使你的代码更易于维护和更新。

CSS是用于设置HTML元素样式、改变其外观和布局的语言。

例如，使用CSS，你可以通过更改其颜色、边框并添加阴影，将一个基本的HTML按钮转变为一个时尚的交互元素。

使用CSS设置样式时，要结合使用选择器、属性和值。

首先，将选择器视为目标，即你想要设置样式的HTML元素，比如一个段落或一个链接。

然后，属性是你想要应用的特定样式，例如颜色或字体大小。

最后，值是你选择的确切设置，比如蓝色或16像素。

让我们付诸实践。

如果你想让网站上的所有链接都是蓝色，并且字体大小为15像素，你可以这样编写CSS代码：A左大括号color blue semicolon font-size 15px semicolon右大括号这里，A是选择器，针对所有链接。

color是属性，blue是你正在应用的值。

使用CSS设计了网站的视觉方面之后，下一步是让它具有交互性。

这时Java Script就登场了。

Java Script是一种使网站具备交互能力的编程语言。

要理解Java Script如何工作，有必要熟悉它的两个基本构建块：语句和表达式。

语句是指示计算机做什么的指令。

它们通常执行一个动作，比如声明一个变量或调用一个函数。

例如，要将字符串“Alice”存储在一个名为name的变量中，你会写let name="Alice"。

表达式是计算机可以评估以产生结果的代码片段，例如数学等式或逻辑比较。

例如，要检查5是否大于3，你会写5>3。

输出结果将是true，因为5确实大于3。

掌握这三种语言仅仅是个开始。

要真正在前端工程领域出类拔萃，你还需要精通关键的技术，比如框架和库。

让我们检视这些重要的技术。

首先关注框架。

框架是预先编写好的代码结构，提供了一种标准化的方式来构建和组织代码。

它们为构建应用程序提供了基础，使开发更快、更高效。

流行的前端框架包括React、Angular和Vue。

接下来，让我们探讨库，库允许工程师控制应用程序的流程和自定义代码，并只选择他们需要的组件。

库是用于执行常见任务的预编写代码的集合。

它们通常包含用于单独解决特定问题的对象和函数。

流行的库包括Node.js、React.js和Vue.js。

这些前端技术使应用程序功能完备，并确保它们具有视觉吸引力且用户友好，使全栈开发者能够创造有凝聚力和引人入胜的用户体验。

在本视频中，我们列举了全栈开发中使用的关键前端技术。

理解并使用这些技术是构建有效且引人入胜的用户界面的关键。

英文原文

In the digital world,front-end technologies help developers build visually appealing and engaging websites and applications,ensuring they are accessible,user-friendly,and work smoothly.

In this video,we will list the key front-end technologies used in full-stack development.

The front-end technologies used in full-stack development include coding languages,frameworks,and libraries.

First,let's start with a definition.

Coding languages are the tools developers use to give instructions to computers.

It's important to understand that computers can only understand binary code,which is a series of ones and zeros.

Most coding languages are designed in a way that makes them simpler for humans to write,but also easier for machines to interpret.

In this way,the computer takes the code you write and translates it into something that it can understand,which is binary code.

Now that we understand what coding languages are and how they work,let's dive into the foundational languages of front-end development,HTML,CSS,and Java Script.

Let's explore each of these languages in more detail,starting with HTML.

HTML is the standard markup language for creating web pages.

It provides all the structure that holds all content together.

Whether it's text,images,or interactive features,HTML organizes elements and gives you browser-specific instructions on how to display them.

Elements are web page components such as paragraphs,links,and media files.

HTML defines these elements using a system of tags.

So how does this work in practice?

Each element in HTML is wrapped in tags that mark the beginning and end of the content.

An element usually consists of a start tag,content,and an end tag.

For instance,to create a paragraph in HTML,you would write it like this.

Open bracket,p,close bracket.

This is a paragraph.

Open bracket slash p,close bracket.

The p tag tells the browser that the text inside should be treated as a paragraph,and the closing p tag denotes that the paragraph has ended.

Now,while some elements are straightforward like this example,others come with additional options known as attributes that provide extra details or settings.

Attributes are often used to specify things like an image's source,a button link,or an element styling.

Let's explore an example of how attributes work within a link element.

A link element starts with an opening tag,A.

This tag stands for anchor,which is used to create hyperlinks.

Next,we add an attribute.

In this case,the href attribute specifies the URL that the link will navigate to when clicked.

For example,href https www.example.com.

The attribute is followed by the content that users will click on.

In this example,visit example website.

Finally,we close the link with the end tag slash a.

This marks the end of the link element.

Now you know how to add elements to a web page,but what if you want to change their size,font,or color?

HTML style attribute can modify font styles and colors,but it's inefficient for large websites.

You need to style each element individually,making updates repetitive and time consuming.

This is where CSS comes in.

CSS separates content from design,allowing you to style multiple elements consistently,making your code easier to maintain and update.

CSS is the language used to style HTML elements,changing their appearance and layout.

For example,using CSS,you can transform a basic HTML button into a stylish interactive element by changing its color,border,and adding shadows.

When styling with CSS,use a combination of selectors,properties,and values.

First,think of the selector as the target,the HTML element you want to style,such as a paragraph or a link.

Then the property is the specific style you want to apply,such as the color or font size.

And finally,the value is the exact setting you choose,like blue or 16 pixels.

Let's put this into practice.

If you want to make all the links on your website blue with a 15 pixel font size,you'd write the CSS code like this.

A,open bracket,color,blue,font size,15 pixels,close bracket.

Here,A is the selector targeting all links.

Color is the property and blue is the value you're applying.

After using CSS to design your website's visual aspects,the next step is to make it interactive.

This is where Java Script comes into play.

Java Script is a programming language that enables interactive websites.

To understand how Java Script works,it's essential to get familiar with its two fundamental building blocks,statements and expressions.

Statements are instructions that tell the computer what to do.

They usually perform an action like declaring a variable or calling a function.

For example,to store the string Alice in a variable called name,you would write let name equal Alice.

An expression is a piece of code that the computer can evaluate to produce a result such as a math equation or a logical comparison.

For example,to check if 5 is greater than 3,you would write 5 greater than symbol 3.

The output would be true because 5 is indeed greater than 3.

Mastering these three languages is just the beginning.

To truly excel in front-end engineering,you need also to become proficient in key technologies such as frameworks and libraries.

Let's examine these essential technologies.

Focusing on frameworks first.

Frameworks are pre-written code structures that offer a standardized way to build and organize code.

They provide a foundation for building applications,making development faster and more efficient.

Popular front-end frameworks include React,Angular,and Vue.

Next,let's explore libraries which allow engineers to control the application's flow and custom code and select only the components they need.

Libraries are collections of pre-written code used to perform common tasks.

They often include objects and functions used individually to solve specific problems.

Popular libraries include Node.js,React.js,and Vue.js.

These front-end technologies make applications functional and ensure they are visually appealing and user-friendly,enabling full-stack developers to create cohesive and engaging user experiences.

In this video,we listed the key front-end technologies used in full-stack development.

Understanding and using these technologies is key to building effective and engaging user interfaces.

01-05.02_基本后端技术

想象一下，你正在开发一个流行的电子商务平台，成千上万的用户同时浏览产品、将商品加入购物车并完成购买。

在幕后，复杂的流程管理着用户请求、与数据库交互，并确保每笔交易都顺畅安全。

要构建这样一个系统，你需要扎实的后端技术基础。

这些是驱动逻辑、数据库交互和API集成的无形引擎，对于你的应用程序无缝运行至关重要。

在本视频中，我们将列举全栈开发中使用的关键后端技术。

在全栈开发中，后端技术处理幕后的繁重工作。

它们管理处理用户请求的服务器端逻辑，确保与数据库的顺畅通信，并将正确的内容传递给前端，所有这些关键功能都能让你的Web应用程序平稳运行。

全栈开发中使用的后端技术包括编码语言、框架和库。

让我们从构成服务器端编程基础的编码语言开始，探讨开发者使用的具体工具。

这些是后端开发者用来编写驱动Web应用程序服务器端代码的核心工具。

后端开发中一些最强大和最常用的语言包括C#、Ruby、Python和Java Script。

C#是一种现代的、面向对象的编程语言，非常适合开发Web API和Windows应用程序。

它因其处理复杂服务器端逻辑的能力而备受推崇。

面向对象编程，简称OOP，是一种将代码组织成对象（即代表应用程序不同部分的自包含单元，例如用户、订单或产品）的编程风格。

这种方法使代码更具模块化、更易于维护和扩展。

当你在构建一个Web应用程序时，你可能会使用C#来创建一个强大的API，处理从用户认证到从数据库检索数据的所有事务。

例如，想象一个在线商店。

C#可用于创建一个API，该API处理客户订单、计算运费，并与库存数据库交互以实时更新库存水平。

正如C#因其在API开发中的稳健性而受到重视一样，Ruby在简化编码过程方面的能力也很突出，使其成为服务器端开发的另一个强大工具。

Ruby是一种多功能的编程语言，以其简洁性和可读性而闻名。

这使得它非常适合用干净、可维护的代码快速构建Web应用程序。

它的语法旨在最大限度地减少复杂性，使开发者能够专注于高效地解决问题。

例如，在开发社交媒体平台时，Ruby允许快速原型设计用户资料、好友连接和实时消息传递等基本功能，同时保持代码清晰。

Python是另一种强大的语言，擅长处理复杂的任务，如业务逻辑、数据分析和机器学习，使其成为后端开发中理想且必不可少的工具。

它经常用于需要大规模数据处理或复杂算法实现的系统中。

例如，想象开发一个需要分析股市趋势并提供实时投资建议的金融应用程序。

Python丰富的库和框架非常适合处理海量数据集并生成准确的预测，确保为用户提供可靠的见解。

Java Script，通常与前端开发联系在一起，由于Node.js的存在，它也是后端的一个强大工具。

Node.js使开发者能够使用Java Script处理诸如处理请求、管理数据库和生成动态内容等任务，并且使用他们已经熟悉的语言。

Node.js是一个运行时环境，使Java Script能够在服务器上运行，从而允许创建快速且可扩展的应用程序。

但什么是运行时环境呢？

运行时环境是一个系统，它为用特定语言编写的代码提供运行和执行任务所需的工具和设置。

Node.js使开发者能够构建快速且可扩展的网络应用程序。

例如，想象开发一个实时聊天应用程序。

Node.js非常适合这个场景，因为它被设计用于同时处理多个连接，允许聊天内容即时更新给所有用户，而不会出现延迟。

虽然编码语言至关重要，但框架和库能帮助开发者更快、更高效地构建应用程序。

让我们从框架开始，检视这些技术，看看它们如何融入后端开发的更大图景中。

框架为开发者提供了一种标准化的方法来构建和组织代码，简化开发过程，并确保项目之间的一致性。

像Express、Django和Ruby on Rails这样的后端框架，为开发者提供了更有效地组织代码和管理服务器端功能的标准方法。

让我们从Express.js开始，逐一探讨这些框架。

Express.js是一个帮助开发者使用Node.js构建网站和应用程序的工具。

它通过处理常见任务（如路由，决定用户如何从一个页面跳转到另一个页面）以及管理服务器端代码，使得创建简单和复杂的Web应用都变得更加容易。

例如，如果你正在构建一个内容管理系统，Express.js可用于管理用户查看、编辑或删除内容所采用的不同路由。

而Django是一个用于Python的高级框架，特别适合数据密集型的应用程序。

它带有用于管理数据库、处理用户认证和保护数据的内置工具。

想象你正在开发一个在线学习平台。

Django可以管理课程、跟踪学生进度并安全地存储用户数据。

它的管理界面还允许你轻松管理平台的内容和用户，而无需编写大量额外的代码。

Ruby on Rails，或简称Rails，是一个帮助开发者使用Ruby构建Web应用程序的框架。

它旨在通过为常见任务提供现成的解决方案来加快开发过程，这样开发者就不必从头开始。

如果你正在构建一个电子商务网站，Rails可以处理从管理产品列表、处理订单到发送确认电子邮件的一切事务。

框架为构建应用程序提供了结构化的方法，而库则为特定任务提供了现成的解决方案。

让我们探讨一下这些预编写代码的集合如何加速开发并为你的项目添加强大的功能。

例如，如果你正在开发一个复杂的动态应用程序，你可能会在后端代码旁边使用像Node.js、React.js或Vue.js这样的库。

这些库通过有效管理服务器和用户界面之间的数据流，使你能够创建流畅、交互式的用户体验。

理解和掌握这些后端技术对于任何全栈开发者来说都是必不可少的。

无论你是处理服务器端逻辑、管理数据库，还是确保你的应用程序可扩展且安全，这些工具都是构建模块，将使你能够创建强大且可靠的Web应用程序。

在本视频中，我们列举了全栈开发中使用的关键后端技术。

理解并使用这些技术是构建健壮的服务器端逻辑和有效数据库管理的关键。

Imagine you're working on a popular e-commerce platform where thousands of users browse products,add items to their cart,and complete purchases all at once.

Behind the scenes,complex processes manage user requests,interact with databases,and ensure that every transaction is smooth and secure.

To build such a system,you need a strong foundation in backend technologies.

These are the invisible engines that power the logic,database interactions,and API integrations essential for your application to function seamlessly.

In this video,we will list the key backend technologies used in full stack development.

In full stack development,backend technologies handle the heavy lifting behind the scenes.

They manage the server-side logic that processes user requests,ensure smooth communication with databases,and delivers the right content to the front-end,all essential functions that keep your web application running smoothly.

The backend technologies used in full-stack development include coding languages,frameworks,and libraries.

Let's explore specific tools developers use,beginning with the coding languages that form the foundation of server-side programming.

These are the core tools that backend developers use to write the code that powers the server-side of a web application.

Some of the most powerful and commonly used languages in backend development include C#,Ruby,Python,and Java Script.

C#is a modern,object-oriented programming language that's well suited for developing web APIs and Windows applications.

It's highly valued for its ability to handle complex server-side logic.

Object-oriented programming,or OOP,is a programming style that organizes code into objects,self-contained units that represent different parts of an application,such as users,orders,or products.

This approach makes the code more modular,maintainable,and scalable.

When you're building a web application,you might use C#to create a robust API that handles everything from user authentication to data retrieval from a database.

For instance,imagine an online store.

C#can be used to create an API that processes customers'orders,calculates shipping costs,and it interacts with the inventory database to update stock levels all in real time.

Just as C#is valued for its robustness in API development,Ruby shines in its ability to simplify the coding process,making it another powerful tool for server-side development.

Ruby is a versatile programming language known for its simplicity and readability.

This makes it ideal for building web applications quickly with clean,maintainable code.

Its syntax is designed to minimize complexity,enabling developers to focus on solving problems efficiently.

For instance,when developing a social media platform,Ruby allows for the rapid prototyping of essential features like user profiles,friend connections,and real-time messaging,all while maintaining code clarity.

Python is another powerful language that excels in handling complex tasks such as business logic,data analysis,and machine learning,making it an ideal and essential tool in backend development.

It is frequently used in systems where large-scale data processing or intricate algorithm implementation is required.

For instance,imagine developing a financial application that needs to analyze stock market trends and provide real-time investment advice.

Python's extensive libraries and frameworks are perfectly suited for processing vast data sets and generating accurate predictions,ensuring reliable insights for users.

Java Script,commonly associated with front-end development,is also a powerful tool for the back-end thanks to Node.js.

Node.js enables developers to use Java Script to handle tasks like processing requests,managing databases,and generating dynamic content,all in a language they're already familiar with.

Node.js is a runtime environment that enables Java Script to run on the server,allowing for the creation of fast and scalable applications.

But what is a runtime environment?

A runtime environment is a system that provides the necessary tools and settings for code written in a particular language to run and perform its tasks.

Node.js enables developers to build fast and scalable network applications.

For example,imagine developing a real-time chat application.

Node.js is perfect for this because it's designed to handle multiple connections simultaneously,allowing the chat to update instantly for all users without delays.

While coding languages are essential,frameworks and libraries help developers build applications faster and more efficiently.

Let's examine these technologies and see how they fit into the bigger picture of backend development,starting with frameworks.

Frameworks provide a developer with a standardized approach to building and organizing code,streamlining the development process,and ensuring consistency across projects.

Backend frameworks like Express,Django,and Ruby on Rails provide developers with standardized methods for organizing code and managing server-side functions more effectively.

Let's explore each of the frameworks starting with Express.js.

Express.js is a tool that helps developers build websites and apps using Node.js.

It makes creating both simple and complex web apps easier by handling common tasks like routing,deciding how users get from one page to another,and managing server-side code.

For example,if you're building a content management system,Express.js could be used to manage the different routes.Users take to view,edit,or delete content.

And Django is a high-level framework for Python that's particularly well suited for data-heavy applications.

It comes with built-in tools for managing databases,handling user authentication,and securing data.

Imagine you're developing an online learning platform.

Django can manage the courses,track student progress,and securely store user data.

Its admin interface also allows you to easily manage the platform's content and users without needing to write a lot of extra code.

Ruby on Rails,or just Rails,is a framework that helps developers build web applications using Ruby.

It's designed to make the process faster by providing ready-made solutions for common tasks so developers don't have to start from scratch.

If you're building an e-commerce site,Rails can handle everything from managing product listings and processing orders to sending confirmation emails.

While frameworks provide a structured approach to building applications,libraries offer ready-made solutions for specific tasks.

Let's explore how these collections of pre-written code can speed up development and add powerful functionality to your projects.

For example,if you're working on a complex dynamic application,you might use libraries like Node.js,React.js,or Vue.js alongside your back-end code.

These libraries allow you to create a smooth,interactive user experience by efficiently managing the flow of data between the server and the user interface.

Understanding and mastering these back-end technologies is essential for any full-stack developer.
