01-06.01_后端项目规划的工具集成

一首优美的交响乐由许多复杂的元素组成。

乐手们准备好他们的乐器，乐谱指引着他们奏出的每一个音符，而指挥则确保一切完美和谐。

后端开发项目就像一首交响乐。

开发者使用他们的工具精心编写代码，即软件世界的音乐。

项目规划工具就像乐谱，概述了需要构建什么以及何时构建。

而集成则处于中心位置，如同指挥一样，确保合适的开发工具和项目规划工具协同工作。

在本视频中，我们将解释如何将项目规划工具与开发工作流程相集成。

集成是组合或协调不同工具或系统，使它们能够相互通信和协同工作的过程。

当项目规划工具与开发工作流程集成时，项目管理和执行会更加紧密地结合，从而使项目更有效、更高效。

让我们探讨一下开发者所依赖的具体工具，并研究从项目规划工具开始，如何通过集成这些工具来简化流程和改善协作。

诸如JIRA、Trello和Asana之类的项目规划工具，是帮助管理任务、跟踪进度以及促进开发团队内部协作的软件应用程序。

这些工具提供了一种结构化的方式来管理任务，支持团队沟通与协作，并跟踪进度和截止日期。

你可以将这些项目规划工具与开发工具集成，以创建无缝的工作流程。

一个关键的开发工具是集成开发环境，或称IDE。

集成开发环境是开发者用于编写、测试和调试代码的主要工具。

它将代码编辑器、编译器、解释器和调试工具等基本工具组合在一个界面中。

一些流行的IDE包括Visual Studio Code、Eclipse和Xcode。

假设一个团队使用JIRA进行项目管理，同时使用Visual Studio Code进行开发。

通过将JIRA与Visual Studio Code集成，开发者可以直接在IDE中管理任务。

这种集成不仅通过将任务管理保留在开发环境中来简化管理，而且还确保更新在跨平台间同步。

开发者工作流程中另一个可以与项目规划工具集成的关键部分是版本控制系统。

版本控制系统是跟踪和管理代码随时间变化的工具，允许多位开发者协作于同一项目而不会覆盖彼此的工作。

将Jira、Trello和Asana等项目管理工具与Git Hub等版本控制系统集成，可以将项目任务与代码更改联系起来。

例如，将代码提交到Git Hub可以自动更新JIRA中相关任务的状态。

这种同步确保所有团队成员都能随时了解最新的开发进展，并实时跟踪进度。

清楚了关键的项目规划工具和开发工具后，我们现在可以将注意力转向将这些工具集成到开发工作流程中的实际步骤。

尽管不同的工具有其特定的集成步骤，但它们通常遵循相同的基本步骤。

首先，定义工作流程，概述关键开发阶段：规划、开发、测试和部署，并确定哪些阶段最能从集成中受益，例如将任务与代码提交关联，或根据代码审查更新任务状态。

接下来，是时候设置集成了。

这一步将根据你使用的工具而有所不同。

然而，有三种常见的集成方式：原生集成、中间件工具和自定义API。

许多工具都带有原生集成，这是软件平台提供的内置连接，允许用户直接与其他应用程序链接，无需额外的编码或第三方软件。

开发者也可以使用中间件工具来集成工具。

中间件工具是作为不同软件应用程序之间桥梁的第三方工具，促进可能没有直接集成能力的系统之间的通信和数据管理。

中间件工具的一些例子包括Microsoft Biz Talk、Flow Middleware Platforms或IBM Web Sphere。

除了原生集成和中间件工具，开发者还可以选择开发和使用自定义API进行集成。

这种方法涉及使用API以特定方式创建定制的连接来链接不同的软件系统，通常需要自定义编码，但提供了最高级别的定制化。

集成的最后一步是设置自动化。

设置集成自动化，使得开发工具中的操作（如提交、拉取请求或成功构建）能自动更新项目规划工具中的任务。

一旦集成完成，它会带来许多好处，包括提高生产力、改善项目跟踪和加强协作。

集成减少了手动更新和数据录入的需求，从而提高了整体生产力。

这使得开发者可以更专注于编码，而不是行政任务，从而加速开发过程。

此外，使用集成工具跟踪项目进度变得更加容易，可以提供项目状态的全面视图。

并且集成工具促进了团队协作，使他们能够实时分享更新和反馈。

在本视频中，我们讨论了将项目规划工具与开发工作流程和工具相集成。

集成这些工具可确保规划和编码之间的无缝过渡，使项目管理更加高效和有效。

英文原文

A beautiful symphony is composed of many intricate elements.

Musicians with their instruments ready to play,sheet music that guides each note they produce,and a conductor who ensures everything is in perfect harmony.

Back-end development projects are like a symphony.

Developers craft the code,the music of the software world,using their tools.

Project planning tools are like sheet music,outlining what needs to be built and when.

At the center,integration acts like the conductor,ensuring that the proper development tools and project planning tools work together.

In this video,we'll explain how to integrate project planning tools with development workflows.

Integration is the process of combining or coordinating different tools or systems so they communicate and work together.

When project planning tools are integrated with development workflows,project management and execution align more closely,leading to more effective and efficient projects.

Let's explore the specific tools that developers rely on and examine how integrating these tools,starting with project planning tools,can streamline processes and improve collaboration.

Project planning tools such as JIRA,Trello,and Asana are software applications that help manage tasks,track progress,and facilitate collaboration within development teams.

These tools provide a structured way to manage tasks,enable team communications and collaboration,and track progress and deadlines.

You can integrate these project planning tools with development tools to create a seamless workflow.

One key development tool is the Integrated Development Environment,or IDE.

An integrated development environment is the primary tool a developer uses to write,test,and debug their code.

It combines essential tools such as a code editor,compiler,interpreter,and debugging tools in a single interface.

Some popular IDEs include Visual Studio Code,Eclipse,and Xcode.

Suppose a team uses JIRA for project management alongside Visual Studio Code for development.

By integrating JIRA with Visual Studio Code,a developer can manage the tasks directly within the IDE.

This integration not only simplifies task management by keeping it within the development environment,but also ensures that updates are synchronized across both platforms.

Another integral part of the developer's workflow that can be integrated with project planning tools are version control systems.

Version control systems are tools that track and manage changes to code over time,allowing multiple developers to collaborate on the same project without overriding one another's work.

Integrating project management tools like Jira,Trello,and Asana with version control systems like Git Hub links project tasks to code changes.

For example,committing code to Git Hub can automatically update the related task status in JIRA.

This synchronization ensures all team members stay informed.of the latest developments and can track progress in real time.

With a clear understanding of the key project planning tools and development tools,we can now turn our attention to the practical steps of integrating these tools within development workflows.

While individual tools differ in specific integration steps,they generally follow the same basic steps.

First,define the workflow,outline key development stages,planning,development,testing,and deployment,and decide which stages would benefit most from integration,such as linking tasks to code,commits,or updating task statuses based on code reviews.

Next,it's time to set up the integration.

This step will be different depending on the tools you use.

However,there are three common ways to integrate tools.

Native integrations,middleware tools,and custom APIs.

Many tools come with native integrations,which are built-in connections provided by software platforms that let users link with other applications directly without the need for additional coding or third-party software.

Developers can also use middleware tools to integrate tools.

Middleware tools are third-party tools that act as a bridge between different software applications,facilitating communications and data management between systems that may not have direct integration capabilities.

Some examples of middleware tools might be Microsoft Biz Talk,Flow Middleware Platforms,or IBM Web Sphere.

In addition to native integrations and middleware tools,developers can also choose to develop and use custom APIs for integrations.

This approach involves creating tailored connections using an API to link different software systems in a specific way,often requiring custom coding,but offering the highest level of customization.

The final step of integration is to set up the automations.

Set up the integration automations so actions in the development tools like commits,pull requests,or successful builds automatically update tasks in the project planning tool.

Once integration is complete,it offers many benefits,including enhanced productivity,better project tracking,and improved collaboration.

Integration reduces the need for manual updates and data entry,enhancing overall productivity.

This allows developers to focus more on coding rather than administrative tasks,thus speeding up the development process.

Also,tracking project progress becomes easier with integrated tools,providing a comprehensive view of the project status.

And integrated tools provide team collaboration,allowing them to share updates and feedback in real time.

In this video,we discussed integrating project planning tools with development workflows and tools.

Integrating these tools ensures seamless transitions between planning and coding,making project management more efficient and effective.

01-06.02_后端开发中集成规划的好处

想象一下，你正在一个复杂的城市里穿行，没有GPS，只有一张纸质地图。

每次到达一个新的十字路口，你都得停下来，展开地图，弄清楚下一步怎么走，同时还得祈祷自己没走错路。

现在，设想一个项目也以类似的方式进行管理。

开发过程的每一步都需要手动更新，与项目经理反复沟通，并且依赖一个只有一个人控制的、过时的任务列表。

如果没有集成工具来实时引导你，你的开发项目就会变得像在没有GPS的城市中穿行一样笨拙且容易延误。

在本视频中，我们将描述将项目规划与开发工具集成的优势。

将JIRA、Asana和Trello等项目规划工具与集成开发环境（也称为IDE）和版本控制系统等开发工具集成，为后端开发项目提供了多重好处。

使用这些集成的团队会体验到更高的生产力、更好的项目跟踪、改进的协作，以及规划与执行之间更紧密的一致性。

让我们从探讨集成如何提高生产力开始。

集成减少了手动更新和数据录入的需求，从而提高了整体生产力。

这使得开发者可以更专注于编码，而不是行政任务，从而加速了开发过程。

它可以自动化某些流程。

例如，当你将一个新API功能的代码提交到像Git Hub这样的版本控制系统时，集成可以自动触发在持续集成工具中启动自动化测试。

集成还有助于在没有人工干预的情况下管理单个任务。

例如，当你提交新API功能的代码时，集成会自动更新你项目管理工具中的任务状态，为你节省时间和精力。

当IDE和项目规划工具之间的集成自动更新你的任务状态时，它也最大限度地减少了上下文切换。

上下文切换是指将你的注意力从一个任务或系统转移到另一个任务或系统，由于需要耗费脑力来重新适应每个新任务，这可能会打断专注力并降低生产力。

自动化使你能够留在你的IDE中，继续工作、测试和提交代码。

这种生产力的提升使你及你的团队能够在更短的时间内完成更多工作，从而加快项目完成速度并提高资源利用效率。

此外，集成工具能确保更好的项目进度跟踪。

当项目管理工具与开发工具集成时，项目进度跟踪变得自动且即时。

例如，当你直接从IDE中将任务标记为完成时，项目管理工具会自动更新进度。

这种集成消除了手动录入的需要，确保项目状态始终反映最新的开发进展，没有任何延迟。

项目经理和团队中的其他人随时都能确切知道任务的状态。

这种实时的项目跟踪允许快速调整和明智的决策，确保项目按计划进行且在范围内。

集成工具还能改善团队协作。

在与你的团队合作时，工具之间的集成意味着无论你是在编写代码、项目经理在更新项目计划，还是市场团队成员在审阅时间表，你们都在访问相同的工具和信息。

这些工具通常也带有集成的沟通功能。

这种集成确保关于特定问题或更新的沟通是即时的、清晰的，并且始终与相关的项目上下文相关联。

这些工具还通过其众多的共享文档功能来改善协作。

最后，集成使团队能够在项目规划和项目执行之间实现更紧密的一致性。

将开发工作与项目计划紧密结合，有助于确保开发工作与项目目标保持一致。

它还允许团队随着项目的进展调整计划，并在规划团队和执行团队之间提供持续的反馈循环。

规划与执行之间更好的一致性确保项目始终专注于其目标，降低范围蔓延的风险，并提高成功的可能性。

在本视频中，我们介绍了将项目规划与开发工具集成的优势，包括改善协作、提高生产力、实时跟踪以及规划与执行之间更好的一致性。

实施这些集成可确保你的开发流程精简高效，从而促成成功的项目成果。（视频由B站【明文传输不】翻译、配音。如果你喜欢计算机编程教学视频请一键三连，谢谢！）

Imagine you're navigating through a complex city without a GPS,only a paper map.

Each time you reach a new intersection,you have to stop,unfold the map,and figure out your next move,all while hoping you haven't taken a wrong turn.

Now,think of a project being managed in a similar way.

Each step of the development process requires manual updates,constant back and forth with the project manager,and an outdated task list that only one person controls.

Without integrated tools to guide you in real time,your development project will become just as cumbersome and prone to delays as navigating in that city was without a GPS.

In this video,we will describe the advantages of integrating project planning with development tools.

Integrating project planning tools like JIRA,Asana,and Trello with development tools such as integrated development environments,also known as IDEs,and version control systems provides multiple benefits for back-end development projects.

Teams that use these integrations experience enhanced productivity and better project tracking,improved collaboration,and increased alignment between planning and execution.

Integration reduces the need for manual updates and data entry,enhancing overall productivity.

This allows developers to focus more on coding rather than administrative tasks,thus speeding up the development process.

It can automate certain processes.

For example,when you commit code for a new API feature to a version control system like Git Hub,an integration can automatically trigger the start of automated tests in a continuous integration tool.

Integrations also help manage individual tasks without human intervention.

For instance,when you commit code for the new API feature,the integration automatically updates the task status in your project management tool,saving you time and effort.

When the integration between the IDE and the project planning tool automatically updates the status of your tasks,it also minimizes context switching.

Context switching refers to shifting your attention from one task or system to another,which can disrupt focus and reduce productivity due to the mental efforts required to reorient to each new task.

The automation allows you to stay within your IDE to continue working,testing,and committing code.

This increased productivity enables you and your team to accomplish more in less time,resulting in faster project completion and more efficient resource use.

Additionally,integrated tools ensure better project progress tracking.

When project management tools are integrated with development tools,project progress tracking becomes automated and instantaneous.

For example,when you mark tasks as complete directly from your IDE,the project management tool automatically updates the progress.

This integration removes the need for manual entry,ensuring that the project status always reflects the latest developments without any delay.

The project manager and others on the team know exactly what the task status is at any given time.

This real-time project tracking can allow for quick adjustments and informed decision-making,ensuring that the project stays on track and within scope.

Integrated tools also improve team collaboration.

When working with your team,integration between tools means that whether you are writing code,the project manager is updating project plans,or a marketing team member is reviewing timelines,you are all accessing the same tools and information.

These tools also often come with integrated communication features.

This integration ensures that the communication about specific issues or updates is immediate,clear,and always linked to the relevant project context.

These tools also improve collaboration with their many shared documentation features.

Finally,integration enables teams to have increased alignment between project planning and project execution.

Closely integrating the development work with the project plan helps ensure that the development work aligns with the project's goals.

It also allows teams to adjust the plan as the project progresses and provides a continuous feedback loop between the planning teams and the execution teams.

Better alignment between planning and execution ensures that the project remains focused on its goals,reduces the risk of scope creep,and improves the chances of success.

01-07.01_后端开发的 Git 基础

想象一下，你是一个繁忙厨房里的厨师，正与团队合作完善一道复杂的菜谱。

你们每个人都贡献自己独特的技术，调整食材，尝试不同的方法。

但是，如果你在任何时候不仅能看到谁添加了什么，还能让多位厨师同时尝试不同的做法，却又不影响原始菜肴，那会怎样呢？

这正是Git在后端开发中让你能够做到的事情。

跟踪变更、实时协作、尝试新想法，同时保持代码库的完整性。

在本视频中，我们将描述基本的Git命令及其在后端开发中的用途。

版本控制至关重要，因为它能跟踪对代码所做的所有更改，允许多位开发者同时协作，支持轻松回退到以前的版本，并让开发者在不影响主代码库的情况下开发新功能或进行修复。

尽管还有其他版本控制系统，但大多数开发者使用Git进行版本控制。

Git是一个分布式版本控制系统，帮助开发者跟踪更改、协作以及维护其代码的历史记录。

可以把Git想象成每位厨师都需要的必备厨房工具，比如刀和砧板，它们帮助厨师创建和管理其烹饪作品的版本。

而Git Hub则像是中心厨房，所有厨师都在那里存放他们的食谱和工具，展示他们的菜肴，并与其他烹饪专家协作。

那么，Git是如何让开发者维护代码历史、跟踪更改并进行协作的呢？

Git涉及仓库和分支的使用。

在Git中，仓库是一个存储空间，用于保存与项目相关的所有文件，包括它们的完整更改历史。

这些仓库保存了代码所有版本的快照，允许开发者跟踪更改、维护历史记录，并在必要时回滚到以前的版本。

接下来，让我们探讨分支。

如果你将主代码视为主干分支，那么当开发者需要添加新代码或对现有代码进行更改时，他们会创建其他分支。

可以把分支想象成复制一个文件夹。

当创建一个新分支时，就如同进行了一次复制。

开发者可以在这个副本中添加、修改和测试文档，而不会影响原件。

一旦他们确信编辑内容正确且稳定，就可以将文件夹中的更改合并回主干分支，用所有新修改来更新它。

开发者借助Git和Git Hub，使用仓库和分支来管理他们的代码。

但是开发者如何与这些工具交互呢？

他们通过执行命令。

开发者通过命令行或像Git Hub这样的应用程序来执行命令。

这些命令包括clone、branch、commit、push和pull。

让我们从clone开始，详细探讨每个命令。

clone命令用于创建开发者正在处理的仓库的本地副本。

这使他们能够开始在自己的机器上使用代码库。

要使用clone命令，你需要输入git空格clone空格，然后是仓库的URL。

例如，如果你的开发者正在为用户认证开发一个新功能，你首先要将现有项目克隆到你的机器上。

一旦你使用clone命令获得了仓库的本地副本，下一步就是创建一个新分支。

分支允许你处理特定的功能或修复，而不会影响主代码库，让你的更改保持隔离，直到它们准备好被合并。

例如，要为你的功能创建一个新分支，你需要输入git空格branch空格，然后是分支名称。

对于你的用户认证功能，你可以使用命令git branch feature-authentication来创建一个分支。

现在，你已准备好开始开发工作。

一旦你编写了一些代码或对现有代码进行了一些更改，你将提交你的代码。

commit命令会捕获项目中更改的快照。

这使你能够跟踪进度，并在需要时恢复到以前的版本。

要使用commit命令，你需要输入git空格commit空格短横线m，然后是用引号括起来的提交信息。

请注意，commit命令包含一条提交信息。

这是开发者撰写有意义的信息来描述对代码库做了什么更改的地方。

例如，在创建了注册表单的基本结构后，你可能会用一条信息“初始化用户注册表单”进行提交。

当你完成所有更改后，可以使用push命令。

push命令用本地更改更新远程仓库，使其他人能够看到并使用这些更新。

要使用push命令，你需要输入git空格push空格，然后是远程仓库的名称，最后是分支名称。

一旦你对新用户注册功能的开发和测试感到满意，下一步就是通过将其推送到Git Hub来分享你的工作。

只需输入git push Origin feature-authentication，即可上传来自feature-authentication分支的所有提交。

第二天，你准备好开始下一个功能的工作，于是执行pull命令。

pull命令用远程仓库的最新更改更新你的本地代码库，确保你拥有最新版本。

要使用pull命令，你需要输入git空格pull空格，然后是远程仓库的名称，最后是分支名称。

你使用pull命令git pull Origin feature-authentication来拉取仓库中代码的最新版本。

拉取最新更新后，你可以返回并使用branch命令从更新后的主分支创建一个新分支，以开始你的下一个功能。

这种工作流程维护了项目的完整性和连续性，使所有开发者能够协作且高效地工作。

今天，我们介绍了基本的Git命令及其在后端开发中的用途，包括clone、branch、commit、push和pull。

理解这些命令对于跟踪更改、与团队成员协作以及维护可靠的代码历史至关重要。

将Git集成到你的开发工作流程中，将显著提高你的效率和生产力。

英文原文

Imagine you are a chef in a bustling kitchen,working with a team to perfect a complex recipe.

Each of you contributes your unique techniques,adjusting ingredients and testing different approaches.

But what if at any moment you could not only see who added what,but also have multiple chefs experiment with variations simultaneously without affecting the original dish?

That's exactly what Git allows you to do in backend development.

Track changes,collaborate in real time,and experiment with new ideas,all while preserving the integrity of your code base.

In this video,we will describe the basic Git commands and their uses in backend development.

Version control is crucial because it tracks all changes made to the code,allows multiple developers to collaborate simultaneously,enables easy reversions to previous versions,and lets developers work on new features or fixes without affecting the main code base.

Though there are other version control systems,most developers use Git for version control.

Git is a distributed version control system that helps developers track changes,collaborate,and maintain the history of their code.

Think of Git as the essential kitchen tools every chef needs,like knives and cutting boards,which help them create and manage versions of their culinary creations.

Git Hub,on the other hand,is like the central kitchen where all chefs store their recipes and tools,showcase their dishes,and collaborate with other culinary experts.

So how does Git enable developers to maintain the history of their code,track changes,and collaborate?

Git involves the use of repositories and branching.

In Git,a repository is a storage space where all files related to a project are kept,including their complete change history.

These repositories hold snapshots of all versions of the code,allowing developers to track changes,maintain history,and roll back to a previous version if necessary.

Next,let's explore branching.

If you think of the main code as the main branch,developers create other branches when they need to add new code or make changes to the existing code.

Think of branching like making a copy of a folder.

And when a new branch is created,it's like making a copy.

Developers can add to,modify,and test the documents in this copy without impacting the original.

Once they're sure the edits are correct and stable,they can merge the changes in the folder back into the main branch,updating it with all the new modifications.

Developers use repositories and branching to manage their code with the help of Git and Git Hub.

But how do developers interact with these tools?

They execute commands.

Developers execute commands from the command line or through an application like Git Hub.

These commands include clone,branch,commit,push,and pull.

Let's explore each of these commands in detail,starting with clone.

The clone command creates a local copy of the repository a developer is working on.

This allows them to start working with the code base on their own machine.

To use the clone command,you would type git space clone space,then the URL of the repository.

For instance,if your developer Developing a new feature for user authentication,you would first clone the existing project to your machine.

Once you have a local copy of the repository with the clone command,the next step is to create a new branch.

Branching allows you to work on specific features or fixes without affecting the main code base,keeping your changes isolated until they're ready to be merged.

For example,to create a new branch for your feature,you would type git space branch space followed by the branch name.

For your user authentication feature,you might create a branch by using the command git branch feature-authentication.

Now,you're ready to get started on the development work.

Once you write some code or make some changes to the existing code,you will commit your code.

The commit command captures a snapshot of the changes in a project.

This allows you to track your progress and revert to a previous version if needed.

To use the commit command,you would type git space commit space dash m,then a commit message in quotes.

Notice that commit commands include a commit message.

This is where developers write a meaningful message to describe what changes were made to the code base.

For example,after creating the basic structure for a registration form,you might commit with a message initialize user registration form.

When you're done with all of the changes,you can use the push command.

The push command updates the remote repository with local changes,allowing others to see and use those updates.

To use the push command,you would type git space push space,then the name of the remote repository,and finally the branch name.

Once you're satisfied with the development and testing of your new user registration feature,the next step is to share your work by pushing it to Git Hub.

Just type git push Origin feature authentication to upload all your commits from the feature authentication branch.

The next day,you're ready to start working on the next feature,so you execute a pull command.

The pull command updates your local code base with the latest changes from the remote repository,ensuring you have the most current version.

To use the pull command,you would type git space pull space,then the name of the remote repository,and finally the branch name.

You use the pull command git pull Origin feature authentication to pull down the latest version of the code in the repository.

After pulling the latest updates,you can go back and use the branch command to create a new branch from the updated main branch to start working on your next feature.

This workflow maintains the integrity and continuity of the project,allowing all developers to work collaboratively and efficiently.

Today,we covered the basic git commands and their uses in backend development,including clone,branch,commit,push,and pull.

Understanding these commands is essential for tracking changes,collaborating with team members,and maintaining a reliable code history.

Integrating Git into your development workflow will significantly enhance your efficiency and productivity.

01-07.02_使用 GitHub 进行协作式后端开发

想象一下，你加入了一个团队，任务是开发一款尖端的远程医疗应用程序。

你负责实现虚拟问诊、患者记录管理和预约安排等功能。

大家共同努力，将创新理念转变为一个功能完备的平台，这可能会彻底改变医疗保健行业。

为了让所有人保持一致，你的团队依赖Git Hub，这是一个强大的版本控制系统，它支持无缝协作、变更跟踪和高效的同行评审。

在本视频中，我们将解释如何使用Git Hub进行协作式后端开发。

Git Hub不仅仅是一个存储代码的地方。

它能在项目的整个生命周期中促进协作，确保每个人的贡献都能无缝地整合在一起。

与开发者孤立工作、最后再尝试合并（这个过程容易引发冲突和错误）不同，Git Hub允许开发者持续观察他们的贡献如何与团队的工作相结合，从而确保无缝集成。

它还允许他们共享资源，如通用库、工具和代码片段，以提高整个项目的效率和一致性，并遵循清晰的编码、审查、测试和构建工作流程。

既然我们已经介绍了为什么Git Hub是一个如此强大的协作工具，现在让我们来探讨开发者实际如何有效利用它进行合作的具体细节。

你可以通过使用Git Hub创建仓库、管理分支、提交拉取请求和执行代码审查，来进行协作和项目管理。

那么，你的项目从哪里开始呢？

一切始于创建仓库，这是你所有代码和协作的大本营。

创建仓库涉及登录Git Hub，导航到仓库部分，然后点击“新建”按钮。

你需要为仓库提供一个名称、一个可选的描述，并选择它是公开还是私有。

例如，你可能通过在Git Hub上创建一个名为Telemedicine app的仓库来开始一个新项目，并将其设为私有以保护敏感的患者数据。

仓库建立好后，你的团队就可以开始开发了。

但是，如何确保多人可以同时处理项目的不同部分，而不会相互覆盖代码呢？

这时就需要管理分支了。

要创建分支，请导航到你在Git Hub上的仓库，并使用“分支”下拉菜单创建一个新分支。

命名约定通常包括正在处理的功能或修复。

对于你的远程医疗应用程序，你可能为新功能“virtual consultations”创建一个分支，以确保主分支保持稳定并随时可以投入生产。

既然你已经在分支上取得了进展，是时候将你的更改合并回主项目了。

为了顺利且有监督地完成此操作，你将提交一个拉取请求。

将更改提交到分支后，导航到仓库中的“拉取请求”选项卡，然后单击“新建拉取请求”。

然后，你可以比较两个分支之间的更改，并提供所提议更改的描述。

例如，你提交一个拉取请求，将你的virtual consultations分支合并到主分支中，并描述你已实现的新虚拟问诊功能。

最后，是时候让同事审查你的工作了。

你发起一个代码审查，涉及团队成员审查新拉取请求中提议的更改，以确保在合并之前的代码质量和功能。

审查拉取请求包括检查代码是否存在错误、是否符合编码标准以及整体功能。

审查者可以对特定代码行发表评论、建议更改，并在满意后批准拉取请求。

你的团队成员审查你的virtual consultations拉取请求，同时你从另一位从事患者记录管理功能的团队成员那里收到代码审查请求。

你注意到代码缺少对敏感患者数据的加密，因此在你的审查中将其作为评论添加。

这种代码审查有助于保持高质量的代码，并确保应用程序符合数据保护标准。

在本视频中，我们解释了如何使用Git Hub进行协作式后端开发，包括创建仓库、管理分支、提交拉取请求和执行代码审查。

通过设置和使用Git Hub仓库，开发团队可以有效地协作、管理代码变更，并维护高质量的代码库。

这提高了生产力，并确保了成功的项目成果。

Imagine stepping into a team tasked with developing a cutting-edge telemedicine application.

It enables collaboration through the lifecycle of a project,making sure that everyone's contributions fit together seamlessly.

Instead of developers working in isolation and trying to merge their work at the end,a process prone to conflict and errors,Git Hub allows developers to continuously observe how their contributions fit with the team's work,ensuring seamless integration.

It also allows them to share resources such as common libraries,tools,and code snippets to increase efficiency and consistency across the project and follow clear workflows for coding,reviewing,testing,and building.

You can both collaborate and manage projects by using Git Hub to create a repository,manage branches,submit pull requests,and conduct code reviews.

You will need to provide a name for your repository,an optional description,and choose whether it will be public or private.
